To address this issue, we propose a Multi-Armed Bandit-based DQA framework (MAB-DQA) to explicitly model the varying importance of multiple implicit aspects in a query. Specifically, MAB-DQA decomposes a query into aspect-aware subqueries and retrieves an aspect-specific candidate set for each. It treats each subquery as an arm and uses preliminary reasoning results from a small number of representative pages as reward signals to estimate aspect utility. Guided by an exploration-exploitation policy, MAB-DQA dynamically reallocates retrieval budgets toward high-value aspects. With the most informative pages and their correlations, MAB-DQA generates the expected results. On four benchmarks, MAB-DQA shows an average improvement of 5%-18% over the state-of-the-art method, consistently enhancing document understanding. Code at https://github.com/ElephantOH/MAB-DQA.

</details>


### [115] [Breaking Block Boundaries: Anchor-based History-stable Decoding for Diffusion Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08964)
*Shun Zou,Yong Wang,Zehui Chen,Lin Chen,Chongyang Tao,Feng Zhao,Xiangxiang Chu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为Anchor-based History-stable Decoding (AHD) 的新型解码策略，用于提升扩散大语言模型（dLLMs）的推理效率与性能，通过动态锚点实时监测token稳定性，实现跨块早解码，在减少80%解码步数的同时提升3.67%性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Semi-AR解码存在固有的块约束，导致跨块稳定token的解码被不必要延迟，限制了dLLMs的效率和性能。

Method: 基于对token稳定性的系统研究，提出AHD方法：利用动态锚点实时监控token的稳定性趋势，一旦token稳定即触发跨块早解码，无需训练，可即插即用。

Result: 在语言、视觉-语言和音频-语言多个领域实验表明，AHD显著提升了推理效率和模型性能；在BBH基准上解码步数减少80%，性能提升3.67%，并逆转了现有加速策略常导致的性能下降问题。

Conclusion: AHD是一种高效、通用且无需训练的动态解码策略，能够有效克服Semi-AR解码的块约束，兼顾推理速度与性能提升，为dLLMs提供了新的优化方向。

Abstract: Diffusion Large Language Models (dLLMs) have recently become a promising alternative to autoregressive large language models (ARMs). Semi-autoregressive (Semi-AR) decoding is widely employed in base dLLMs and advanced decoding strategies due to its superior performance. However, our observations reveal that Semi-AR decoding suffers from inherent block constraints, which cause the decoding of many cross-block stable tokens to be unnecessarily delayed. To address this challenge, we systematically investigate the identification of stable tokens and present three key findings: (1) naive lookahead decoding is unreliable, (2) token stability closely correlates with convergence trend, and (3) historical information is isolated. Building on these insights, we propose Anchor-based History-stable Decoding (AHD), a training-free, plug-and-play dynamic decoding strategy. Specifically, AHD monitors the stability trend of tokens in real time through dynamic anchors. Once a token reaches stability, it initiates early cross-block decoding to enhance efficiency and performance. Extensive experiments across language, vision-language, and audio-language domains demonstrate that AHD simultaneously improves both performance and inference efficiency. Notably, AHD effectively reverses the performance degradation typically observed in existing advanced decoding acceleration strategies. For instance, on the BBH benchmark, our approach reduces decoding steps by 80% while improving performance by 3.67%.

</details>


### [116] [Confident in a Confidence Score: Investigating the Sensitivity of Confidence Scores to Supervised Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08974)
*Lorenzo Jaime Yu Flores,Cesare Spinoso di-Piano,Jackie Chi Kit Cheung*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究发现，监督微调（SFT）会降低语言模型中置信度分数与输出质量之间的相关性，导致不确定性量化效果下降，因此需要开发对微调更鲁棒的置信度度量方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了理解监督微调（SFT）如何影响语言模型中置信度分数与输出质量之间的相关性，从而提升不确定性量化技术在实际应用中的可靠性。

Method: 通过分析不同置信度分数在SFT前后的相关性变化，并结合案例研究探讨其在下游任务中的影响，识别导致相关性下降的因素。

Result: 发现SFT后多种置信度分数与输出质量的相关性均下降，这种退化可能源于输出与训练分布相似性等因素对置信度的影响，而非输出质量本身。案例研究表明，这种误关联会削弱置信度分数在下游任务中的实用性。

Conclusion: 置信度指标不能开箱即用，必须经过测试；未来需开发对微调更鲁棒的不确定性度量方法。

Abstract: Uncertainty quantification is a set of techniques that measure confidence in language models. They can be used, for example, to detect hallucinations or alert users to review uncertain predictions. To be useful, these confidence scores must be correlated with the quality of the output. However, recent work found that fine-tuning can affect the correlation between confidence scores and quality. Hence, we investigate the underlying behavior of confidence scores to understand its sensitivity to supervised fine-tuning (SFT). We find that post-SFT, the correlation of various confidence scores degrades, which can stem from changes in confidence scores due to factors other than the output quality, such as the output's similarity to the training distribution. We demonstrate via a case study how failing to address this miscorrelation reduces the usefulness of the confidence scores on a downstream task. Our findings show how confidence metrics cannot be used off-the-shelf without testing, and motivate the need for developing metrics which are more robust to fine-tuning.

</details>


### [117] [Quantisation Reshapes the Metacognitive Geometry of Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08976)
*Jon-Paul Cacioli*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 模型量化会重构而非均匀降低大语言模型的领域元认知效率，研究发现M-ratio在不同精度格式下领域分布显著变化，但Type-2 AUROC保持稳定，表明变化源于M-ratio归一化而非判别能力本身；基于领域条件训练提升元认知的尝试未成功，因诊断结果在格式间无法迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究模型量化对大语言模型在不同知识领域中元认知效率的影响，并验证通过领域条件训练改善元认知的有效性。

Method: 在Q5_K_M和f16两种精度下评估Llama-3-8B-Instruct对3000个问题的表现，分析四个知识领域的M-ratio和Type-2 AUROC变化；采用预注册实验设计，实施信心放大SFT及对照训练，使用10000次自助重采样检验假设。

Result: M-ratio在不同量化格式下领域分布完全不相关（Spearman rho = 0.00），如艺术与文学从监控最差变为最佳，地理则相反；但Type-2 AUROC高度稳定（rho = 1.00）；尽管SFT成功改变置信度分布，但未提升meta-d'，因诊断特征不跨格式迁移。

Conclusion: 依赖领域级M-ratio的系统存在对推理格式的未检视依赖，而使用AUROC_2的系统更稳健；当前基于M-ratio的元认知诊断在实际部署中需谨慎对待。

Abstract: We report that model quantisation restructures domain-level metacognitive efficiency in LLMs rather than degrading it uniformly. Evaluating Llama-3-8B-Instruct on the same 3,000 questions at Q5_K_M and f16 precision, we find that M-ratio profiles across four knowledge domains are uncorrelated between formats (Spearman rho = 0.00). Arts & Literature moves from worst-monitored (M-ratio = 0.606 at Q5_K_M) to best-monitored (1.542 at f16). Geography moves from well-monitored (1.210) to under-monitored (0.798). However, Type-2 AUROC profiles are perfectly stable across formats (rho = 1.00), localising the restructuring to the M-ratio normalisation rather than the underlying discrimination signal. This finding emerged from a pre-registered attempt to improve metacognition through domain-conditional training. We prescribed confidence-amplification SFT for the diagnosed weak domain, with matched-budget agnostic and wrong-prescription controls. All four confirmatory hypotheses were null (10,000 bootstrap resamples, seed = 42). The training successfully reshaped confidence distributions, doubling the NLP gap in Science from 0.076 to 0.152, but did not improve meta-d' because the diagnostic profile did not transfer across formats. Any system relying on domain-level M-ratio profiles has an unexamined dependency on inference format. Systems using AUROC_2 are safer. We release all code, pre-registrations, and trial-level data.

</details>


### [118] [Testing the Assumptions of Active Learning for Translation Tasks with Few Samples](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08977)
*Lorenzo Jaime Yu Flores,Cesare Spinoso di-Piano,Ori Ernst,David Ifeoluwa Adelani,Jackie Chi Kit Cheung*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了在少量样本（100-500）情况下，主动学习（AL）策略在语言生成任务中为何未能优于随机采样的原因，发现传统AL优化的信息性和多样性与模型性能无显著相关性，而训练样本顺序和预训练数据交互等因素影响更大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近年来AL方法在语言生成任务中使用少量样本时表现不佳，作者旨在探究其背后的核心假设是否成立。

Method: 通过分析不同AL策略下样本的信息性、多样性与模型最终测试性能之间的相关性，并考察样本排序及与预训练数据的交互影响。

Result: 发现信息性和多样性与测试性能无显著相关性，而样本顺序和与预训练数据的交互对性能影响更大。

Conclusion: 现有的AL策略在极少量样本下失效，未来的方法需考虑样本顺序和预训练数据交互等新因素。

Abstract: Active learning (AL) is a training paradigm for selecting unlabeled samples for annotation to improve model performance on a test set, which is useful when only a limited number of samples can be annotated. These algorithms often work by optimizing for the informativeness and diversity of the training data to be annotated. Recent work found that AL strategies fail to outperform random sampling on various language generation tasks when using 100-500 samples. To understand AL's poor performance when only using few samples, we investigate whether the core assumptions underlying AL strategies hold. We find that neither the informativeness nor diversity of the training data, which AL strategies optimize for, are correlated with test set performance. Instead, factors like the ordering of the training samples and interactions with pre-training data have a larger impact on performance. This suggests that future AL methods must take these factors into account in order to work with very few samples.

</details>


### [119] [PerMix-RLVR: Preserving Persona Expressivity under Verifiable-Reward Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08986)
*Jihwan Oh,Soowon Oh,Murad Aghazada,Minchan Jeong,Sungnyun Kim,Se-Young Yun*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为PerMix-RLVR的新型训练策略，旨在解决大语言模型在不同角色提示下的鲁棒性与角色保真度之间的权衡问题。通过在训练阶段引入角色混合的强化学习方法，该方法在提升对有害角色变化的鲁棒性的同时，保留了所需的角色表达能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在推理时搜索最优角色提示效率低下，且对角色敏感性缺乏理解；基于结果的优化虽能提升任务性能，但会损害角色表达的真实性。

Method: 采用强化学习与可验证奖励（RLVR）框架，在训练中引入角色混合策略（PerMix-RLVR），平衡模型对不同角色的适应性与任务表现。

Result: PerMix-RLVR在MATH500上将角色稳定性评分（PSS）较RLVR提升了+21.2%，同时在PersonaGym上角色保真度提高+11.4%。

Conclusion: PerMix-RLVR有效缓解了角色鲁棒性与表达保真度之间的冲突，实现了在保持任务性能的同时更好遵循所需角色的行为引导。

Abstract: Persona prompting has been widely adopted to steer large language models (LLMs) behavior and improve their instruction performance by assigning specific characters. However, identifying an optimal persona is time-consuming, and its impact on output quality remains poorly understood. Prior work has mainly addressed this issue at the prompt level via inference-time strategies, incurring additional computation. In this work, we avoid inference-time prompt search by tackling persona sensitivity during training, aiming to train models that adapt their behavior to diverse personas while preserving task performance. In particular, we find that reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) systematically reduces sensitivity to persona prompts, but also reveals an inherent trade-off of outcome-based optimization: while RLVR improves robustness on tasks with verifiable goals, it can also degrade persona expressivity when needed, e.g., in-character role-playing. To address this limitation, we propose PerMix-RLVR, a persona-mixed RLVR strategy that mitigates the persona robustness-fidelity trade-off, preserving strong robustness to harmful persona variation while enabling faithful persona adoption when required. Concretely, PerMix-RLVR improves persona stability score (PSS) over RLVR by +21.2% on MATH500, while also enhancing persona fidelity by +11.4% on PersonaGym.

</details>


### [120] [ASTRA: Adaptive Semantic Tree Reasoning Architecture for Complex Table Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08999)
*Xiaoke Guo,Songze Li,Zhiqiang Liu,Zhaoyan Gong,Yuanxiang Liu,Huajun Chen,Wen Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出ASTRA框架，通过自适应语义树和双模式推理提升大模型在复杂表格问答中的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有表格序列化方法在结构表达、语义适应性和推理透明度方面存在不足，难以有效支持大语言模型处理复杂表格问答任务。

Method: 提出ASTRA框架，包含AdaSTR模块用于构建具有层次依赖的逻辑语义树，并采用自适应机制优化不同规模表格的构造策略；以及DuTR模块实现基于树搜索的文本导航与符号化代码执行相结合的双模式推理。

Result: 在多个复杂表格问答基准测试中达到最先进性能。

Conclusion: ASTRA有效解决了表格序列化中的结构忽视、表示差距和推理不透明问题，显著提升了大语言模型对复杂表格的理解与推理能力。

Abstract: Table serialization remains a critical bottleneck for Large Language Models (LLMs) in complex table question answering, hindered by challenges such as structural neglect, representation gaps, and reasoning opacity. Existing serialization methods fail to capture explicit hierarchies and lack schema flexibility, while current tree-based approaches suffer from limited semantic adaptability. To address these limitations, we propose ASTRA (Adaptive Semantic Tree Reasoning Architecture) including two main modules, AdaSTR and DuTR. First, we introduce AdaSTR, which leverages the global semantic awareness of LLMs to reconstruct tables into Logical Semantic Trees. This serialization explicitly models hierarchical dependencies and employs an adaptive mechanism to optimize construction strategies based on table scale. Second, building on this structure, we present DuTR, a dual-mode reasoning framework that integrates tree-search-based textual navigation for linguistic alignment and symbolic code execution for precise verification. Experiments on complex table benchmarks demonstrate that our method achieves state-of-the-art (SOTA) performance.

</details>


### [121] [Towards Linguistically-informed Representations for English as a Second or Foreign Language: Review, Construction and Application](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09008)
*Wenxi Li,Xihao Wang,Weiwei Sun*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于构式语法的新型英语二语/外语（ESFL）语言资源，包含1643个标注句子，旨在捕捉ESFL独特的句法-语义映射，并展示了其在二语习得研究中的应用潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ESFL资源不足以充分表征二语语言系统的独特性，缺乏对句法-语义接口的深入建模，因此需要一种知识密集型、理论驱动的表示方法。

Method: 基于建构主义语言理论，以构式为基本分析单位，构建了一个涵盖1643个标注ESFL句子的金标准句法-语义语料库（sembank），并用于检验语言生态位假设的初步研究。

Result: 成功构建了一个高质量的ESFL句法-语义资源库，能够有效捕捉多种ESFL语言现象，并通过实证研究验证了其在第二语言习得研究中的实用性。

Conclusion: 该研究为ESFL提供了一个理论坚实、标注精细的语言资源，推动了对非母语英语变体的系统性理解，并为未来二语习得和计算语言学研究提供了重要工具。

Abstract: The widespread use of English as a Second or Foreign Language (ESFL) has sparked a paradigm shift: ESFL is not seen merely as a deviation from standard English but as a distinct linguistic system in its own right. This shift highlights the need for dedicated, knowledge-intensive representations of ESFL. In response, this paper surveys existing ESFL resources, identifies their limitations, and proposes a novel solution. Grounded in constructivist theories, the paper treats constructions as the fundamental units of analysis, allowing it to model the syntax--semantics interface of both ESFL and standard English. This design captures a wide range of ESFL phenomena by referring to syntactico-semantic mappings of English while preserving ESFL's unique characteristics, resulting a gold-standard syntactico-semantic resource comprising 1643 annotated ESFL sentences. To demonstrate the sembank's practical utility, we conduct a pilot study testing the Linguistic Niche Hypothesis, highlighting its potential as a valuable tool in Second Language Acquisition research.

</details>


### [122] [CONDESION-BENCH: Conditional Decision-Making of Large Language Models in Compositional Action Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09029)
*Yeonjun Hwang,Sungyong Park,Minju Kim,Dongha Lee,Jinyoung Yeo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了CONDESION-BENCH，一个用于评估大语言模型在组合动作空间中条件决策能力的基准，通过引入多层次约束和基于oracle的评估，更严格地衡量模型的决策质量和约束遵循能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有决策基准假设动作来自预定义集合且忽略可行性约束，无法反映现实世界中动作的组合结构和实际限制，因此需要更贴近真实场景的评估框架。

Method: 设计了一个新的基准CONDESION-BENCH，将动作定义为对决策变量的分配，并在变量、上下文和分配层面引入显式约束条件，采用基于oracle的方法评估决策质量和约束遵守情况。

Result: 该基准能够更全面地评估大语言模型在复杂、受约束环境下的决策能力，揭示其在条件理解和组合动作生成方面的表现。

Conclusion: CONDESION-BENCH为评估大语言模型作为高风险领域决策支持工具提供了更严谨的测试平台，推动模型向更可靠的实际应用发展。

Abstract: Large language models have been widely explored as decision-support tools in high-stakes domains due to their contextual understanding and reasoning capabilities. However, existing decision-making benchmarks rely on two simplifying assumptions: actions are selected from a finite set of pre-defined candidates, and explicit conditions restricting action feasibility are not incorporated into the decision-making process. These assumptions fail to capture the compositional structure of real-world actions and the explicit conditions that constrain their validity. To address these limitations, we introduce CONDESION-BENCH, a benchmark designed to evaluate conditional decision-making in compositional action space. In CONDESION-BENCH, actions are defined as allocations to decision variables and are restricted by explicit conditions at the variable, contextual, and allocation levels. By employing oracle-based evaluation of both decision quality and condition adherence, we provide a more rigorous assessment of LLMs as decision-support tools.

</details>


### [123] [Anchored Sliding Window: Toward Robust and Imperceptible Linguistic Steganography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09066)
*Ruiyi Yan,Shiao Meng,Yugo Murawaki*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于锚定滑动窗口（ASW）的鲁棒语言模型隐写框架，通过引入桥接上下文和自蒸馏策略，在提升文本质量和隐写鲁棒性方面显著优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于语言模型的文本隐写方法在传输过程中对文本修改极为敏感，且限制上下文窗口的方法会严重损害生成文本的质量，因此需要一种既能保持高质量又能增强鲁棒性的新框架。

Method: 提出了锚定滑动窗口（ASW）框架，在滑动窗口中固定提示词和一个桥接上下文，使模型能补偿被排除的上下文信息；将桥接上下文的优化建模为提示蒸馏的一种变体，并结合自蒸馏策略进行改进。

Result: 实验表明，ASW在多种设置下均显著优于基线方法，提升了文本质量、不可感知性和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: ASW框架通过锚定关键上下文并利用自蒸馏优化桥接内容，有效解决了语言模型隐写中的脆弱性问题，实现了高质量与强鲁棒性的平衡。

Abstract: Linguistic steganography based on language models typically assumes that steganographic texts are transmitted without alteration, making them fragile to even minor modifications. While previous work mitigates this fragility by limiting the context window, it significantly compromises text quality. In this paper, we propose the anchored sliding window (ASW) framework to improve imperceptibility and robustness. In addition to the latest tokens, the prompt and a bridge context are anchored within the context window, encouraging the model to compensate for the excluded tokens. We formulate the optimization of the bridge context as a variant of prompt distillation, which we further extend using self-distillation strategies. Experiments show that our ASW significantly and consistently outperforms the baseline method in text quality, imperceptibility, and robustness across diverse settings. The code is available at github.com/ryehr/ASW_steganography.

</details>


### [124] [NyayaMind- A Framework for Transparent Legal Reasoning and Judgment Prediction in the Indian Legal System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09069)
*Parjanya Aditya Shukla,Shubham Kumar Nigam,Debtanu Datta,Balaramamahanthi Deepak Patnaik,Noel Shallum,Pradeep Reddy Vanga,Saptarshi Ghosh,Arnab Bhattacharya*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: NyayaMind是一个开源框架，旨在为印度司法系统实现透明且可扩展的法律推理，通过结合检索、推理和验证机制来提升法院判决预测与解释的质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了使法院判决预测与解释系统在司法或法律研究中具有实际应用价值，需要不仅具备高预测性能，还能生成符合司法实践的透明、结构化法律推理。

Method: NyayaMind包含检索模块和预测模块：检索模块采用RAG管道从大规模法律语料中识别相关法规和先例；预测模块使用针对印度法律领域微调的推理型大语言模型，生成包括问题、论点、理由和判决在内的结构化输出。

Result: 实验结果和专家评估表明，NyayaMind在解释质量与证据对齐方面显著优于现有方法。

Conclusion: NyayaMind在构建可信的AI辅助法律决策支持系统方面迈出了重要一步。

Abstract: Court Judgment Prediction and Explanation (CJPE) aims to predict a judicial decision and provide a legally grounded explanation for a given case based on the facts, legal issues, arguments, cited statutes, and relevant precedents. For such systems to be practically useful in judicial or legal research settings, they must not only achieve high predictive performance but also generate transparent and structured legal reasoning that aligns with established judicial practices. In this work, we present NyayaMind, an open-source framework designed to enable transparent and scalable legal reasoning for the Indian judiciary. The proposed framework integrates retrieval, reasoning, and verification mechanisms to emulate the structured decision-making process typically followed in courts. Specifically, NyayaMind consists of two main components: a Retrieval Module and a Prediction Module. The Retrieval Module employs a RAG pipeline to identify legally relevant statutes and precedent cases from large-scale legal corpora, while the Prediction Module utilizes reasoning-oriented LLMs fine-tuned for the Indian legal domain to generate structured outputs including issues, arguments, rationale, and the final decision. Our extensive results and expert evaluation demonstrate that NyayaMind significantly improves the quality of explanation and evidence alignment compared to existing CJPE approaches, providing a promising step toward trustworthy AI-assisted legal decision support systems.

</details>


### [125] [Hierarchical Alignment: Enforcing Hierarchical Instruction-Following in LLMs through Logical Consistency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09075)
*Shu Yang,Zihao Zhou,Di Wang,Wenda Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为Neuro-Symbolic Hierarchical Alignment (NSHA)的方法，用于处理大型语言模型在多源指令下的优先级冲突问题，通过推理时的约束求解和训练时的知识蒸馏，提升模型在复杂指令环境中的行为一致性与任务效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要关注对抗性攻击中的指令优先级，忽视了实际应用中常见的良性指令冲突；模型需在保障安全的同时维持任务性能和行为一致性。

Method: 提出NSHA框架，在推理阶段采用求解器引导的推理将指令解析建模为约束满足问题，在训练阶段通过自动构建的监督信号将求解决策蒸馏至模型参数中。

Result: 在规则遵循、任务执行、工具使用和安全性等多个任务上验证了NSHA的有效性，涵盖单轮与多轮交互场景，显著提升了存在指令冲突时的性能，同时在无冲突场景下保持良好效用。

Conclusion: NSHA能够有效处理多层次、异构来源的指令冲突，在保障安全的前提下提升了模型的实用性和一致性，适用于复杂的现实应用场景。

Abstract: Large language models increasingly operate under multiple instructions from heterogeneous sources with different authority levels, including system policies, user requests, tool outputs, and retrieved context. While prior work on instruction hierarchy highlights the importance of respecting instruction priorities, it mainly focuses on adversarial attacks and overlooks the benign but common instruction conflicts that arise in real-world applications. In such settings, models must not only avoid security violations but also preserve task utility and behavioral consistency when instructions partially or implicitly conflict. We propose Neuro-Symbolic Hierarchical Alignment (NSHA) for hierarchical instruction-following by explicitly modeling and enforcing instruction priorities. At inference time, we introduce solver-guided reasoning that formulates instruction resolution as a constraint satisfaction problem, enabling the model to derive a maximally consistent set of applicable instructions under hierarchical constraints. At training time, NSHA distills solver-based decisions into model parameters using automatically constructed supervision. We evaluate our approach on rule following, task execution, tool use, and safety, covering both single-turn and multi-turn interactions, and show that NSHA significantly improves performance under such conflicts while maintaining competitive utility in reference settings.

</details>


### [126] [Interactive ASR: Towards Human-Like Interaction and Semantic Coherence Evaluation for Agentic Speech Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09121)
*Peng Wang,Yanqiao Zhu,Zixuan Jiang,Qinyuan Chen,Xingjian Zhao,Xipeng Qiu,Wupeng Wang,Zhifu Gao,Xiangang Li,Kai Yu,Xie Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于大语言模型（LLM）的智能体框架，用于实现语义感知的交互式自动语音识别（ASR），通过引入LLM-as-a-Judge评估机制和多轮反馈优化显著提升了识别结果的语义保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的ASR系统主要依赖词错误率（WER）作为评价指标，忽略了语义正确性，且缺乏对交互式纠错机制的研究。为此，本文旨在探索能够模拟人类交互行为、提升语义准确性的新型ASR框架。

Method: 提出一个以大语言模型为驱动的智能体框架，利用LLM-as-a-Judge作为语义感知的评估指标，并通过多轮交互反馈机制实现识别结果的迭代优化，支持英语、中文及混合语种场景。

Result: 在GigaSpeech、WenetSpeech和ASRU 2019混杂语种测试集上进行了广泛实验，客观与主观评估均表明该框架能有效提升语义保真度和交互纠错能力。

Conclusion: 所提出的基于LLM的智能体框架为实现更自然、更具交互性的语音识别系统提供了新方向，推动ASR从传统token级匹配向语义级理解演进。

Abstract: Recent years have witnessed remarkable progress in automatic speech recognition (ASR), driven by advances in model architectures and large-scale training data. However, two important aspects remain underexplored. First, Word Error Rate (WER), the dominant evaluation metric for decades, treats all words equally and often fails to reflect the semantic correctness of an utterance at the sentence level. Second, interactive correction-an essential component of human communication-has rarely been systematically studied in ASR research. In this paper, we integrate these two perspectives under an agentic framework for interactive ASR. We propose leveraging LLM-as-a-Judge as a semantic-aware evaluation metric to assess recognition quality beyond token-level accuracy. Furthermore, we design an LLM-driven agent framework to simulate human-like multi-turn interaction, enabling iterative refinement of recognition outputs through semantic feedback. Extensive experiments are conducted on standard benchmarks, including GigaSpeech (English), WenetSpeech (Chinese), the ASRU 2019 code-switching test set. Both objective and subjective evaluations demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed framework in improving semantic fidelity and interactive correction capability. We will release the code to facilitate future research in interactive and agentic ASR.

</details>


### [127] [Prototype-Regularized Federated Learning for Cross-Domain Aspect Sentiment Triplet Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09123)
*Zongming Cai,Jianhang Tang,Zhenyong Zhang,Jinghui Qin,Kebing Jin,Hankz Hankui Zhuo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于原型的跨域联邦学习框架PCD-SpanProto，用于解决ASTE任务中的领域异质性和数据隐私问题，通过交换类级原型提升模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ASTE方法通常孤立训练于单一数据集，无法捕捉跨域共享特征，且受数据隐私限制难以集中聚合数据，因此需要一种兼顾隐私保护与跨域知识迁移的分布式学习框架。

Method: 设计了PCD-SpanProto框架，采用原型正则化的联邦学习机制，在客户端间交换类级原型而非完整模型参数；引入加权性能感知聚合策略和对比正则化模块，增强全局原型在领域异质下的鲁棒性，并提升类内紧凑性和类间可分性。

Result: 在四个ASTE数据集上进行了广泛实验，结果表明该方法优于基线模型，同时降低了通信成本，验证了其在跨域知识迁移和分布式学习中的有效性。

Conclusion: PCD-SpanProto有效实现了隐私保护下的跨域ASTE学习，通过原型交换与正则化机制，在减少通信开销的同时提升了多领域下的模型性能。

Abstract: Aspect Sentiment Triplet Extraction (ASTE) aims to extract all sentiment triplets of aspect terms, opinion terms, and sentiment polarities from a sentence. Existing methods are typically trained on individual datasets in isolation, failing to jointly capture the common feature representations shared across domains. Moreover, data privacy constraints prevent centralized data aggregation. To address these challenges, we propose Prototype-based Cross-Domain Span Prototype extraction (PCD-SpanProto), a prototype-regularized federated learning framework to enable distributed clients to exchange class-level prototypes instead of full model parameters. Specifically, we design a weighted performance-aware aggregation strategy and a contrastive regularization module to improve the global prototype under domain heterogeneity and the promotion between intra-class compactness and inter-class separability across clients. Extensive experiments on four ASTE datasets demonstrate that our method outperforms baselines and reduces communication costs, validating the effectiveness of prototype-based cross-domain knowledge transfer.

</details>


### [128] [Think Less, Know More: State-Aware Reasoning Compression with Knowledge Guidance for Efficient Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09150)
*Yi Sui,Chaozhuo Li,Dawei Song*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一种新的推理压缩框架STACK，通过状态感知和知识引导实现细粒度的链式思维压缩，在数学推理任务中显著减少了推理长度并提高了准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的思维链压缩方法难以在准确性和效率之间取得平衡，且缺乏对冗余和推理偏差的细粒度适应能力。

Method: 构建在线长短对比样本，结合检索增强的指导，动态切换知识引导压缩和自提示压缩，并引入基于答案收敛的早停机制；采用结合PPO和DPO的奖励差异驱动训练策略。

Result: 在三个数学推理基准上，平均响应长度减少59.9%，准确率比现有方法提高4.8个百分点。

Conclusion: STACK框架能有效识别不同推理状态下的冗余，实现高效、准确的推理压缩，显著优于现有方法。

Abstract: Large Reasoning Models (LRMs) achieve strong performance on complex tasks by leveraging long Chain-of-Thought (CoT), but often suffer from overthinking, leading to excessive reasoning steps and high inference latency. Existing CoT compression methods struggle to balance accuracy and efficiency, and lack fine-grained, step-level adaptation to redundancy and reasoning bias. Therefore, we propose State-Aware Reasoning Compression with Knowledge Guidance (STACK), a framework that performs step-wise CoT compression by explicitly modeling stage-specific redundancy sources and integrating with a retrieval-augmented guidance. STACK constructs online long-short contrastive samples and dynamically switches between knowledge-guided compression for uncertain or biased reasoning state and self-prompted compression for overly long but confident state, complemented by an answer-convergence-based early stopping mechanism to suppress redundant verification. We further propose a reward-difference-driven training strategy by combining Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO) and Direct Preference Optimization (DPO), enabling models to learn state-conditioned compression strategies. Experiments on three mathematical reasoning benchmarks show that STACK achieves a superior accuracy-efficiency balance, reducing average response length by 59.9% while improving accuracy by 4.8 points over existing methods.

</details>


### [129] [Facet-Level Tracing of Evidence Uncertainty and Hallucination in RAG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09174)
*Passant Elchafei,Monorama Swain,Shahed Masoudian,Markus Schedl*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向问答任务的细粒度诊断框架，通过将问题分解为原子推理要素，并结合检索相关性和自然语言推断来评估证据的充分性和忠实性，揭示了RAG系统中幻觉主要源于生成过程中对检索证据的错误整合而非检索本身。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的RAG评估方法多集中在答案或段落级别，难以深入理解模型在生成过程中如何利用检索到的证据；为此，作者希望引入更细粒度的分析手段以识别幻觉产生的根本原因。

Method: 提出一种基于‘要素×块’矩阵的细粒度诊断框架，将问题分解为原子推理要素，并在三种推理模式（严格RAG、软RAG和纯LLM生成）下评估证据使用情况，结合检索相关性和基于自然语言推断的忠实度评分进行分析。

Result: 在医疗问答和HotpotQA数据集上对GPT、Gemini和LLaMA等模型的实验表明，幻觉更多源于证据整合失败而非检索失败，常见失败模式包括证据缺失、证据错位和先验知识覆盖。

Conclusion: RAG系统中的幻觉问题关键在于生成阶段对检索证据的不当使用，而非检索准确性不足；细粒度的要素级分析能有效揭示传统答案级评估无法发现的系统性错误模式。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) aims to reduce hallucination by grounding answers in retrieved evidence, yet hallucinated answers remain common even when relevant documents are available. Existing evaluations focus on answer-level or passage-level accuracy, offering limited insight into how evidence is used during generation. In this work, we introduce a facet-level diagnostics framework for QA that decomposes each input question into atomic reasoning facets. For each facet, we assess evidence sufficiency and grounding using a structured Facet x Chunk matrix that combines retrieval relevance with natural language inference-based faithfulness scores. To diagnose evidence usage, we analyze three controlled inference modes: Strict RAG, which enforces exclusive reliance on retrieved evidence; Soft RAG, which allows integration of retrieved evidence and parametric knowledge; and LLM-only generation without retrieval. Comparing these modes enables thorough analysis of retrieval-generation misalignment, defined as cases where relevant evidence is retrieved but not correctly integrated during generation. Across medical QA and HotpotQA, we evaluate three open-source and closed-source LLMs (GPT, Gemini, and LLaMA), providing interpretable diagnostics that reveal recurring facet-level failure modes, including evidence absence, evidence misalignment, and prior-driven overrides. Our results demonstrate that hallucinations in RAG systems are driven less by retrieval accuracy and more by how retrieved evidence is integrated during generation, with facet-level analysis exposing systematic evidence override and misalignment patterns that remain hidden under answer-level evaluation.

</details>


### [130] [Do LLMs Follow Their Own Rules? A Reflexive Audit of Self-Stated Safety Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09189)
*Avni Mittal*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了符号-神经一致性审计（SNCA）框架，用于衡量大语言模型在安全策略上的声明与行为之间的一致性，发现多个前沿模型存在系统性差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准测试无法衡量模型是否理解并执行其自身声明的安全边界，因此需要一种新方法来评估模型的自我一致性。

Method: 通过结构化提示提取模型自述的安全规则，将其形式化为类型化谓词（绝对、条件、适应性），并通过与危害基准的确定性比较来测量行为合规性。

Result: 在4个前沿模型、45类危害和47,496次观察中发现：声称绝对拒绝的模型仍常响应有害请求；推理模型自我一致性最高但有29%类别未明确政策；跨模型规则类型一致性极低（11%）。

Conclusion: 大语言模型“所说”与“所做”之间的差距是可测量的且依赖于架构，应将反思性一致性审计作为行为基准的补充。

Abstract: LLMs internalize safety policies through RLHF, yet these policies are never formally specified and remain difficult to inspect. Existing benchmarks evaluate models against external standards but do not measure whether models understand and enforce their own stated boundaries. We introduce the Symbolic-Neural Consistency Audit (SNCA), a framework that (1) extracts a model's self-stated safety rules via structured prompts, (2) formalizes them as typed predicates (Absolute, Conditional, Adaptive), and (3) measures behavioral compliance via deterministic comparison against harm benchmarks. Evaluating four frontier models across 45 harm categories and 47,496 observations reveals systematic gaps between stated policy and observed behavior: models claiming absolute refusal frequently comply with harmful prompts, reasoning models achieve the highest self-consistency but fail to articulate policies for 29% of categories, and cross-model agreement on rule types is remarkably low (11%). These results demonstrate that the gap between what LLMs say and what they do is measurable and architecture-dependent, motivating reflexive consistency audits as a complement to behavioral benchmarks.

</details>


### [131] [ScheMatiQ: From Research Question to Structured Data through Interactive Schema Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09237)
*Shahar Levy,Eliya Habba,Reshef Mintz,Barak Raveh,Renana Keydar,Gabriel Stanovsky*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: ScheMatiQ是一个利用大语言模型自动生成问答所需结构化数据的系统，支持跨领域研究问题的分析，并已开源发布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统手动设计标注模式并标注语料库耗时且易出错，难以满足多学科自然语言研究问题对结构化证据的需求。

Method: 提出ScheMatiQ系统，通过调用大语言模型，根据研究问题和语料库自动生成标注模式和结构化数据库，并提供可交互的网页界面供用户引导和修改信息抽取过程。

Result: 与法律和计算生物学领域的专家合作验证，ScheMatiQ能够生成支持实际分析的结构化数据输出。

Conclusion: ScheMatiQ有效减少了人工标注负担，提升了从大规模文本中提取结构化证据的效率和准确性，具备跨学科应用潜力。

Abstract: Many disciplines pose natural-language research questions over large document collections whose answers typically require structured evidence, traditionally obtained by manually designing an annotation schema and exhaustively labeling the corpus, a slow and error-prone process. We introduce ScheMatiQ, which leverages calls to a backbone LLM to take a question and a corpus to produce a schema and a grounded database, with a web interface that lets steer and revise the extraction. In collaboration with domain experts, we show that ScheMatiQ yields outputs that support real-world analysis in law and computational biology. We release ScheMatiQ as open source with a public web interface, and invite experts across disciplines to use it with their own data. All resources, including the website, source code, and demonstration video, are available at: www.ScheMatiQ-ai.com

</details>


### [132] [EthicMind: A Risk-Aware Framework for Ethical-Emotional Alignment in Multi-Turn Dialogue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09265)
*Jiawen Deng,Wei Li,Wentao Zhang,Ziyun Jiao,Fuji Ren*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了EthicMind框架，用于在多轮对话中实现伦理与情感的联合对齐，通过在每一轮中联合分析伦理风险和用户情绪，生成兼顾伦理指导与情感共鸣的回复，且无需额外训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对话模型通常孤立处理共情与伦理安全问题，难以适应多轮交互中动态变化的伦理风险与用户情绪，可能导致在敏感场景中的失败。

Method: 将伦理-情感对齐建模为显式的回合级决策问题，提出EthicMind框架，在推理时联合分析伦理风险信号与用户情绪，规划响应策略并生成上下文敏感的回复，无需额外训练。引入基于风险分层的多轮评估协议与情境感知的用户模拟程序进行评测。

Result: 实验结果表明，EthicMind在高风险和道德模糊情境下，相比基线模型能提供更一致的伦理指导和情感参与。

Conclusion: EthicMind能够有效实现多轮对话中伦理与情感的动态对齐，提升对话系统在敏感场景下的安全性与共情能力。

Abstract: Intelligent dialogue systems are increasingly deployed in emotionally and ethically sensitive settings, where failures in either emotional attunement or ethical judgment can cause significant harm. Existing dialogue models typically address empathy and ethical safety in isolation, and often fail to adapt their behavior as ethical risk and user emotion evolve across multi-turn interactions. We formulate ethical-emotional alignment in dialogue as an explicit turn-level decision problem, and propose \textsc{EthicMind}, a risk-aware framework that implements this formulation in multi-turn dialogue at inference time. At each turn, \textsc{EthicMind} jointly analyzes ethical risk signals and user emotion, plans a high-level response strategy, and generates context-sensitive replies that balance ethical guidance with emotional engagement, without requiring additional model training. To evaluate alignment behavior under ethically complex interactions, we introduce a risk-stratified, multi-turn evaluation protocol with a context-aware user simulation procedure. Experimental results show that \textsc{EthicMind} achieves more consistent ethical guidance and emotional engagement than competitive baselines, particularly in high-risk and morally ambiguous scenarios.

</details>


### [133] [Task-Aware LLM Routing with Multi-Level Task-Profile-Guided Data Synthesis for Cold-Start Scenarios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09377)
*Hui Liu,Bin Zou,Kecheng Chen,Jie Liu,Wenya Wang,Haoliang Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于多级任务特征引导的数据合成框架和任务类型感知的路由器TRouter，以解决大语言模型在冷启动场景下路由性能泛化能力差的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有路由器在缺乏领域内训练数据的冷启动场景下泛化能力差，难以有效平衡用户指定的成本与性能需求。

Method: 构建层次化任务分类体系，通过多级任务特征引导的数据合成生成多样化问答对，并设计TRouter模型，利用潜在任务类型变量建模查询条件下的成本与性能，结合合成任务分类的先验正则化进行路由决策。

Result: 在多个基准测试上验证了所提数据合成框架能有效缓解冷启动问题，TRouter在冷启动和领域内设置下均实现了高效的LLM路由性能。

Conclusion: 该方法提升了路由器在冷启动场景下的适应性和性能，为大语言模型的动态路由提供了更鲁棒的解决方案。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) exhibit substantial variability in performance and computational cost across tasks and queries, motivating routing systems that select models to meet user-specific cost-performance trade-offs. However, existing routers generalize poorly in cold-start scenarios where in-domain training data is unavailable. We address this limitation with a multi-level task-profile-guided data synthesis framework that constructs a hierarchical task taxonomy and produces diverse question-answer pairs to approximate the test-time query distribution. Building on this, we introduce TRouter, a task-type-aware router approach that models query-conditioned cost and performance via latent task-type variables, with prior regularization derived from the synthesized task taxonomy. This design enhances TRouter's routing utility under both cold-start and in-domain settings. Across multiple benchmarks, we show that our synthesis framework alleviates cold-start issues and that TRouter delivers effective LLM routing.

</details>


### [134] [Automated Instruction Revision (AIR): A Structured Comparison of Task Adaptation Strategies for LLM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09418)
*Solomiia Bilyk,Volodymyr Getmanskyi,Taras Firman*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了基于规则归纳的自动指令修订（AIR）方法，用于利用少量任务特定样例来适应大语言模型。研究比较了包括提示优化、基于检索的方法和微调在内的多种适应策略，并发现不同任务中最佳方法各异：AIR在标签重映射分类上表现最优，KNN检索在闭卷问答中领先，而微调在结构化抽取和事件顺序推理中占优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了有效利用有限的任务特定样例提升大语言模型在下游任务上的表现，探索一种可解释且高效的适应方法。

Method: 提出自动指令修订（AIR），通过规则归纳生成可解释的指令规则以适配任务；并在涵盖知识注入、结构提取、标签重映射和逻辑推理的多样化基准套件上与其他适应策略进行比较。

Result: 在五个基准测试中，AIR在标签重映射分类任务上表现最好或接近最佳；KNN检索在闭卷问答中效果最优；微调在结构化提取和事件顺序推理任务中表现最强。

Conclusion: 适应方法的效果具有显著的任务依赖性，没有单一方法在所有任务中都占优；当任务行为可通过简洁、可解释的指令规则捕获时，AIR最具潜力，而在依赖特定知识或标注规律的任务中，检索和微调更为有效。

Abstract: This paper studies Automated Instruction Revision (AIR), a rule-induction-based method for adapting large language models (LLMs) to downstream tasks using limited task-specific examples. We position AIR within the broader landscape of adaptation strategies, including prompt optimization, retrieval-based methods, and fine-tuning. We then compare these approaches across a diverse benchmark suite designed to stress different task requirements, such as knowledge injection, structured extraction, label remapping, and logical reasoning. The paper argues that adaptation performance is strongly task-dependent: no single method dominates across all settings. Across five benchmarks, AIR was strongest or near-best on label-remapping classification, while KNN retrieval performed best on closed-book QA, and fine-tuning dominated structured extraction and event-order reasoning. AIR is most promising when task behavior can be captured by compact, interpretable instruction rules, while retrieval and fine-tuning remain stronger in tasks dominated by source-specific knowledge or dataset-specific annotation regularities.

</details>


### [135] [UIPress: Bringing Optical Token Compression to UI-to-Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09442)
*Dasen Dai,Shuoqi Li,Ronghao Chen,Huacan Wang,Biao Wu,Qizhen Lan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: UIPress是一种用于UI-to-Code生成的轻量级学习压缩模块，通过在视觉Transformer编码器和大语言模型解码器之间引入光学压缩，显著提升视觉token效率，在保持高性能的同时实现更快的首 token 生成速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的视觉token压缩方法在UI-to-Code任务中效率低下，无法有效减少预填充延迟或适应UI截图中非均匀的信息密度；同时缺乏针对该任务的编码端学习压缩方法。

Method: 提出UIPress，一种插入在冻结ViT编码器与LLM解码器之间的轻量级学习压缩模块，结合深度可分离卷积、元素引导的空间重加权和Transformer refinement，将约6,700个视觉token压缩至固定的256个，并采用LoRA微调解码器以弥合表示差距。

Result: 在Design2Code数据集上，UIPress将视觉token压缩至256时达到0.8127的CLIP分数，比未压缩基线提升+7.5%，优于最强推理时方法+4.6%，并实现9.1倍的首token生成速度提升。

Conclusion: UIPress是首个应用于UI-to-Code任务的编码端学习压缩方法，显著提升了生成效率与性能，且仅增加约2170万可训练参数（占基础模型0.26%），具有高实用价值。

Abstract: UI-to-Code generation requires vision-language models (VLMs) to produce thousands of tokens of structured HTML/CSS from a single screenshot, making visual token efficiency critical. Existing compression methods either select tokens at inference time using task-agnostic heuristics, or zero out low-attention features without actually shortening the sequence -- neither truly reduces prefill latency or adapts to the non-uniform information density of UI screenshots. Meanwhile, optical (encoder-side learned) compression has shown strong results for document OCR, yet no prior work has adapted this paradigm to UI-to-Code generation. We propose UIPress, a lightweight learned compression module inserted between the frozen ViT encoder and the LLM decoder of Qwen3-VL-8B. UIPress combines depthwise-separable convolutions, element-guided spatial reweighting, and Transformer refinement to compress ${\sim}$6{,}700 visual tokens to a fixed budget of 256. Together with Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) on the decoder to bridge the representation gap, the entire system adds only ${\sim}$21.7M trainable parameters (0.26\% of the 8B base model). Under a fair comparison on the same base model against four baselines on Design2Code, UIPress at 256 tokens achieves a CLIP score of 0.8127, outperforming the uncompressed baseline by +7.5\% and the strongest inference-time method by +4.6\%, while delivering 9.1$\times$ time-to-first-token speedup. To the best of our knowledge, UIPress is the first encoder-side learned compression method for the UI-to-Code task.

</details>


### [136] [Across the Levels of Analysis: Explaining Predictive Processing in Humans Requires More Than Machine-Estimated Probabilities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09466)
*Sathvik Nair,Colin Phillips*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文从Marr的分析层次出发，批判并扩展了关于语言模型和语言处理的两个观点，探讨了预测在语言处理中的核心作用以及大语言模型对心理语言学发展的贡献，并提出了结合两者优势的未来研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了更深入理解语言模型在语言处理中的作用，作者希望从Marr的多层次分析框架出发，重新审视当前关于语言预测和大语言模型在心理语言学中作用的观点。

Method: 采用Marr的三个分析层次（计算、算法和实现）作为理论框架，对现有观点进行批判性分析，并提出概念上的扩展和未来研究方向。

Result: 作者指出，虽然预测在语言处理中确实重要，但不能完全代表语言理解的整体过程；同时强调，尽管大语言模型推动了心理语言学的发展，但其本身并不能替代传统的心理语言学模型。

Conclusion: 未来的研究应融合大语言模型的强大预测能力与心理语言学模型对认知机制的精细刻画，以实现对人类语言处理更全面的理解。

Abstract: Under the lens of Marr's levels of analysis, we critique and extend two claims about language models (LMs) and language processing: first, that predicting upcoming linguistic information based on context is central to language processing, and second, that many advances in psycholinguistics would be impossible without large language models (LLMs). We further outline future directions that combine the strengths of LLMs with psycholinguistic models.

</details>


### [137] [RecaLLM: Addressing the Lost-in-Thought Phenomenon with Explicit In-Context Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09494)
*Kyle Whitecross,Negin Rahimi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: RecaLLM是一种通过交替推理与上下文检索来增强长上下文利用的推理语言模型，采用低开销的约束解码机制，在不依赖超长训练数据的情况下显著提升了长上下文任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语言模型在长上下文推理中存在“失思”（lost-in-thought）问题，即推理步骤越长，上下文检索能力越弱，限制了测试时的扩展性，本文旨在解决推理与检索之间的脱节问题。

Method: 提出RecaLLM，将推理与显式上下文检索交错进行，并引入一种零额外开销的约束解码机制，支持证据片段的逐字复制，提升生成的准确性；在多样化的词汇和语义检索任务上进行训练。

Result: RecaLLM在RULER和HELMET两个长上下文基准上显著优于基线模型，且在高达128K token的上下文窗口中持续获得增益，而训练样本最长仅10K token。

Conclusion: 通过联合优化推理与检索过程，RecaLLM有效缓解了长上下文中的信息遗忘问题，为不依赖长上下文训练数据提升模型性能提供了可行路径。

Abstract: We propose RecaLLM, a set of reasoning language models post-trained to make effective use of long-context information. In-context retrieval, which identifies relevant evidence from context, and reasoning are deeply intertwined: retrieval supports reasoning, while reasoning often determines what must be retrieved. However, their interaction remains largely underexplored. In preliminary experiments on several open-source LLMs, we observe that in-context retrieval performance substantially degrades even after a short reasoning span, revealing a key bottleneck for test-time scaling that we refer to as lost-in-thought: reasoning steps that improve performance also make subsequent in-context retrieval more challenging. To address this limitation, RecaLLM interleaves reasoning with explicit in-context retrieval, alternating between reasoning and retrieving context information needed to solve intermediate subproblems. We introduce a negligible-overhead constrained decoding mechanism that enables verbatim copying of evidence spans, improving the grounding of subsequent generation. Trained on diverse lexical and semantic retrieval tasks, RecaLLM achieves strong performance on two long-context benchmarks, RULER and HELMET, significantly outperforming baselines. Notably, we observe consistent gains at context windows of up to 128K tokens using training samples of at most 10K tokens, far shorter than those used by existing long-context approaches, highlighting a promising path toward improving long-context performance without expensive long-context training data.

</details>


### [138] [BERT-as-a-Judge: A Robust Alternative to Lexical Methods for Efficient Reference-Based LLM Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09497)
*Hippolyte Gisserot-Boukhlef,Nicolas Boizard,Emmanuel Malherbe,Céline Hudelot,Pierre Colombo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于BERT的评估方法BERT-as-a-Judge，用于解决大语言模型生成结果评估中词汇匹配方法与人类判断相关性差的问题。该方法在保持轻量级的同时，能够有效评估语义正确性，性能接近大型LLM裁判模型，且具备良好的可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的生成式模型评估多依赖于刚性的词汇匹配方法，容易将模型的格式遵循能力误判为问题解决能力，且现有基于大语言模型的裁判方法计算开销大。因此需要一种高效、准确且低成本的评估方案。

Method: 提出BERT-as-a-Judge，使用编码器架构，在合成标注的问-候选-参考三元组上进行轻量训练，以实现对生成答案语义正确性的评估，并在15个下游任务和36个模型上进行大规模实证研究验证其有效性。

Result: BERT-as-a-Judge在多项任务上显著优于词汇匹配基线，性能与更大的LLM裁判模型相当，但计算成本更低；实验表明其对输出表述变化具有鲁棒性，且与人类判断高度一致。

Conclusion: BERT-as-a-Judge提供了一种高效、可靠且可扩展的评估方法，平衡了准确性与计算成本，适用于实际场景中的大语言模型输出评估，并推动了评估技术的普及化。

Abstract: Accurate evaluation is central to the large language model (LLM) ecosystem, guiding model selection and downstream adoption across diverse use cases. In practice, however, evaluating generative outputs typically relies on rigid lexical methods to extract and assess answers, which can conflate a model's true problem-solving ability with its compliance with predefined formatting guidelines. While recent LLM-as-a-Judge approaches mitigate this issue by assessing semantic correctness rather than strict structural conformity, they also introduce substantial computational overhead, making evaluation costly. In this work, we first systematically investigate the limitations of lexical evaluation through a large-scale empirical study spanning 36 models and 15 downstream tasks, demonstrating that such methods correlate poorly with human judgments. To address this limitation, we introduce BERT-as-a-Judge, an encoder-driven approach for assessing answer correctness in reference-based generative settings, robust to variations in output phrasing, and requiring only lightweight training on synthetically annotated question-candidate-reference triplets. We show that it consistently outperforms the lexical baseline while matching the performance of much larger LLM judges, providing a compelling tradeoff between the two and enabling reliable, scalable evaluation. Finally, through extensive experimentation, we provide detailed insights into BERT-as-a-Judge's performance to offer practical guidance for practitioners, and release all project artifacts to foster downstream adoption.

</details>


### [139] [You Can't Fight in Here! This is BBS!](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09501)
*Richard Futrell,Kyle Mahowald*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过Norm和Claudette的对话，探讨了现代语言模型是否能为语言科学提供重要洞见，并批判了‘字符串统计稻草人’和‘现状即极限’两种误解，主张在人工智能时代拓展语言科学研究范式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 澄清对语言模型的常见误解，推动语言科学在AI时代的更广泛发展。

Method: 通过虚构对话引入多学科讨论，分析语言模型与人类语言研究的关系。

Result: 识别出两个关键误解：认为语言模型仅是字符串统计工具而不具备语言能力（字符串统计稻草人），以及认为当前语言模型研究已达到极限（现状即极限假设）。

Conclusion: 应建立更包容和扩展的研究框架，整合语言模型研究以深化对人类语言和模型本身的科学理解。

Abstract: Norm, the formal theoretical linguist, and Claudette, the computational language scientist, have a lovely time discussing whether modern language models can inform important questions in the language sciences. Just as they are about to part ways until they meet again, 25 of their closest friends show up -- from linguistics, neuroscience, cognitive science, psychology, philosophy, and computer science. We use this discussion to highlight what we see as some common underlying issues: the String Statistics Strawman (the mistaken idea that LMs can't be linguistically competent or interesting because they, like their Markov model predecessors, are statistical models that learn from strings) and the As Good As it Gets Assumption (the idea that LM research as it stands in 2026 is the limit of what it can tell us about linguistics). We clarify the role of LM-based work for scientific insights into human language and advocate for a more expansive research program for the language sciences in the AI age, one that takes on the commentators' concerns in order to produce a better and more robust science of both human language and of LMs.

</details>


### [140] [Many Ways to Be Fake: Benchmarking Fake News Detection Under Strategy-Driven AI Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09514)
*Xinyu Wang,Sai Koneru,Wenbo Zhang,Wenliang Zheng,Saksham Ranjan,Sarah Rajtmajer*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了MANYFAKE，一个包含6,798篇合成假新闻文章的基准数据集，用于评估现有假新闻检测模型在处理混合真实与虚假信息时的有效性。研究发现，即使是最先进的模型在面对微妙且精心设计的虚假信息时仍表现脆弱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代假新闻越来越多地通过人机协作生成，其中战略性错误嵌入于整体准确的叙述中，构成现实且严重的威胁，但现有基准对此类混合真相案例代表不足。

Method: 构建了一个名为MANYFAKE的合成基准数据集，使用多种策略驱动的提示流程生成不同类型的假新闻文章，并利用该数据集评估了一系列最先进的假新闻检测器。

Result: 实验结果显示，即使是具备高级推理能力的模型，在完全虚构的故事上已接近性能饱和，但在虚假信息较为微妙、经过优化并与真实信息交织的情况下仍然表现脆弱。

Conclusion: 当前的假新闻检测模型在应对复杂、策略性嵌入的虚假信息方面仍有显著局限，需进一步研究以提升其鲁棒性和识别能力。

Abstract: Recent advances in large language models (LLMs) have enabled the large-scale generation of highly fluent and deceptive news-like content. While prior work has often treated fake news detection as a binary classification problem, modern fake news increasingly arises through human-AI collaboration, where strategic inaccuracies are embedded within otherwise accurate and credible narratives. These mixed-truth cases represent a realistic and consequential threat, yet they remain underrepresented in existing benchmarks. To address this gap, we introduce MANYFAKE, a synthetic benchmark containing 6,798 fake news articles generated through multiple strategy-driven prompting pipelines that capture many ways fake news can be constructed and refined. Using this benchmark, we evaluate a range of state-of-the-art fake news detectors. Our results show that even advanced reasoning-enabled models approach saturation on fully fabricated stories, but remain brittle when falsehoods are subtle, optimized, and interwoven with accurate information.

</details>


### [141] [Case-Grounded Evidence Verification: A Framework for Constructing Evidence-Sensitive Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09537)
*Soroosh Tayebi Arasteh,Mehdi Joodaki,Mahshad Lotfinia,Sven Nebelung,Daniel Truhn*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于案例的证据验证框架，通过生成显式支持样本和受控非支持样本来加强监督，从而提升模型对证据依赖性的建模能力，在放射学任务中验证了该方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型在证据支持判断中常因监督信号弱、证据与主张关联松散以及评估方式无法直接检验证据依赖性而表现不佳，因此需要一种能明确编码证据因果作用的监督机制。

Method: 提出案例基础的证据验证框架，设计自动生成支持样本与语义控制的非支持样本（如反事实错误状态和主题相关负例）的监督构建方法，并在放射学领域实例化该框架以训练验证器模型。

Result: 所训练的验证器显著优于仅使用案例或仅使用证据的基线模型，在正确证据下表现稳健，而在移除或替换证据时性能急剧下降，显示出真实的证据依赖性；该行为可迁移到未见的证据文章和外部案例分布，但在证据来源变化时性能下降，且对主干模型选择敏感。

Conclusion: 证据接地的主要瓶颈不仅是模型容量，更在于缺乏能够编码证据因果作用的监督方式，强监督信号对于实现真正的证据依赖至关重要。

Abstract: Evidence-grounded reasoning requires more than attaching retrieved text to a prediction: a model should make decisions that depend on whether the provided evidence supports the target claim. In practice, this often fails because supervision is weak, evidence is only loosely tied to the claim, and evaluation does not test evidence dependence directly. We introduce case-grounded evidence verification, a general framework in which a model receives a local case context, external evidence, and a structured claim, and must decide whether the evidence supports the claim for that case. Our key contribution is a supervision construction procedure that generates explicit support examples together with semantically controlled non-support examples, including counterfactual wrong-state and topic-related negatives, without manual evidence annotation. We instantiate the framework in radiology and train a standard verifier on the resulting support task. The learned verifier substantially outperforms both case-only and evidence-only baselines, remains strong under correct evidence, and collapses when evidence is removed or swapped, indicating genuine evidence dependence. This behavior transfers across unseen evidence articles and an external case distribution, though performance degrades under evidence-source shift and remains sensitive to backbone choice. Overall, the results suggest that a major bottleneck in evidence grounding is not only model capacity, but the lack of supervision that encodes the causal role of evidence.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [142] [LEGO: Latent-space Exploration for Geometry-aware Optimization of Humanoid Kinematic Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08636)
*Jihwan Yoon,Taemoon Jeong,Jeongeun Park,Chanwoo Kim,Jaewoon Kwon,Yonghyeon Lee,Kyungjae Lee,Sungjoon Choi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种数据驱动的机器人形态与运动学联合优化新范式，通过从现有机械设计中学习设计空间并利用人类运动数据定义损失函数，实现自动化机器人设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统机器人形态设计依赖人工直觉，缺乏系统性基础，且设计空间庞大、任务特定损失函数难以构建，亟需一种减少人为干预的自动化设计方法。

Method: 采用基于旋量理论的关节轴表示和等距流形学习，从现有机械设计中学习并构建紧凑、保几何的类人上身设计潜在空间，并结合运动重定向与Procrustes分析，直接从人类运动数据定义损失函数，使用无梯度优化进行设计优化。

Result: 成功构建了一个可处理的潜在设计空间，实现了在该空间中的机器人形态与运动学联合优化，生成了新颖且符合人体运动特性的机器人设计。

Conclusion: 该方法为数据驱动的机器人设计提供了原则性框架，验证了利用现有设计和人类运动数据可有效指导新型机器人设计的自动发现。

Abstract: Designing robot morphologies and kinematics has traditionally relied on human intuition, with little systematic foundation. Motion-design co-optimization offers a promising path toward automation, but two major challenges remain: (i) the vast, unstructured design space and (ii) the difficulty of constructing task-specific loss functions. We propose a new paradigm that minimizes human involvement by (i) learning the design search space from existing mechanical designs, rather than hand-crafting it, and (ii) defining the loss directly from human motion data via motion retargeting and Procrustes analysis. Using screw-theory-based joint axis representation and isometric manifold learning, we construct a compact, geometry-preserving latent space of humanoid upper body designs in which optimization is tractable. We then solve design optimization in this latent space using gradient-free optimization. Our approach establishes a principled framework for data-driven robot design and demonstrates that leveraging existing designs and human motion can effectively guide the automated discovery of novel robot design.

</details>


### [143] [Generative Simulation for Policy Learning in Physical Human-Robot Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08664)
*Junxiang Wang,Xinwen Xu,Tiancheng Wu,Julian Millan,Nir Pechuk,Zackory Erickson*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种零样本的“text2sim2real”生成式仿真框架，利用大语言模型和视觉-语言模型从自然语言指令自动生成多样化的物理人机交互场景，并实现从仿真到真实环境的零样本迁移，在辅助抓挠和洗澡任务中取得了超过80%的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于缺乏大规模训练数据，自主物理人机交互（pHRI）系统的发展受到限制，现有方法难以泛化到真实复杂场景，因此需要一种能自动构建多样化仿真环境并高效生成训练数据的方法。

Method: 提出一个基于大语言模型（LLMs）和视觉-语言模型（VLMs）的生成式仿真流程，能够从高级自然语言提示中自动生成软体人体模型、场景布局和机器人运动轨迹；利用生成的合成数据，在分段点云上训练基于视觉的模仿学习策略，并实现零样本sim-to-real迁移。

Result: 在两个物理辅助任务（抓挠和洗澡）上的用户研究显示，所训练的策略在未见过的真实场景中成功率达到80%以上，并能应对非预期的人体动作，表现出良好的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 本文首次实现了面向pHRI应用的生成式仿真流水线，实现了仿真环境构建、数据收集与策略学习的自动化，为解决真实世界pHRI数据稀缺问题提供了新途径。

Abstract: Developing autonomous physical human-robot interaction (pHRI) systems is limited by the scarcity of large-scale training data to learn robust robot behaviors for real-world applications. In this paper, we introduce a zero-shot "text2sim2real" generative simulation framework that automatically synthesizes diverse pHRI scenarios from high-level natural-language prompts. Leveraging Large Language Models (LLMs) and Vision-Language Models (VLMs), our pipeline procedurally generates soft-body human models, scene layouts, and robot motion trajectories for assistive tasks. We utilize this framework to autonomously collect large-scale synthetic demonstration datasets and then train vision-based imitation learning policies operating on segmented point clouds. We evaluate our approach through a user study on two physically assistive tasks: scratching and bathing. Our learned policies successfully achieve zero-shot sim-to-real transfer, attaining success rates exceeding 80% and demonstrating resilience to unscripted human motion. Overall, we introduce the first generative simulation pipeline for pHRI applications, automating simulation environment synthesis, data collection, and policy learning. Additional information may be found on our project website: https://rchi-lab.github.io/gen_phri/

</details>


### [144] [Task-Aware Bimanual Affordance Prediction via VLM-Guided Semantic-Geometric Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08726)
*Fabian Hahne,Vignesh Prasad,Georgia Chalvatzaki,Jan Peters,Alap Kshirsagar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于视觉-语言模型的分层框架，用于任务感知的双臂操作，通过语义推理实现物体交互区域与手臂分配的联合优化，在真实场景中显著提升了任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在双臂操作中无法有效结合任务语义进行可操作区域预测和手臂分配，缺乏对任务意图的联合推理能力。

Method: 将多视角RGB-D数据融合为一致的3D场景表示，生成全局抓取候选，并利用视觉-语言模型（VLM）查询任务相关的可操作区域及对象对应的手臂分配，实现几何有效性与任务语义的统一。

Result: 在包含四类九项真实世界任务的双臂平台上进行评估，相比几何与语义基线方法，在面向任务的抓取中实现了更高的任务成功率。

Conclusion: 显式的语义推理在可操作性分析与手臂分配中的联合建模有助于提升非结构化环境中双臂操作的可靠性。

Abstract: Bimanual manipulation requires reasoning about where to interact with an object and which arm should perform each action, a joint affordance localization and arm allocation problem that geometry-only planners cannot resolve without semantic understanding of task intent. Existing approaches either treat affordance prediction as coarse part segmentation or rely on geometric heuristics for arm assignment, failing to jointly reason about task-relevant contact regions and arm allocation. We reframe bimanual manipulation as a joint affordance localization and arm allocation problem and propose a hierarchical framework for task-aware bimanual affordance prediction that leverages a Vision-Language Model (VLM) to generalize across object categories and task descriptions without requiring category-specific training. Our approach fuses multi-view RGB-D observations into a consistent 3D scene representation and generates global 6-DoF grasp candidates, which are then spatially and semantically filtered by querying the VLM for task-relevant affordance regions on each object, as well as for arm allocation to the individual objects, thereby ensuring geometric validity while respecting task semantics. We evaluate our method on a dual-arm platform across nine real-world manipulation tasks spanning four categories: parallel manipulation, coordinated stabilization, tool use, and human handover. Our approach achieves consistently higher task success rates than geometric and semantic baselines for task-oriented grasping, demonstrating that explicit semantic reasoning over affordances and arm allocation helps enable reliable bimanual manipulation in unstructured environments.

</details>


### [145] [Toward Hardware-Agnostic Quadrupedal World Models via Morphology Conditioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08780)
*Mohamad H. Danesh,Chenhao Li,Amin Abyaneh,Anas Houssaini,Kirsty Ellis,Glen Berseth,Marco Hutter,Hsiu-Chin Lin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种可泛化的四足世界模型（QWM），通过显式引入机器人形态学参数，实现对不同四足机器人形态的零样本迁移控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界模型因过拟合特定机器人形态而难以泛化，轻微的硬件变化即需重新训练，限制了其在真实场景中的应用。

Method: 设计了一个显式编码机器人工程参数（如肢体长度、质量）的物理形态编码器，并结合奖励归一化机制，使模型能够解耦环境动力学与机器人形态，从而支持跨形态的零样本迁移。

Result: 首次实现了世界模型在四足机器人形态间的零样本泛化能力，可在未见形态上直接进行有效控制，但其泛化能力限于形态分布内的插值。

Conclusion: QWM是迈向形态条件化世界模型的重要一步，为基于学习的具身智能提供了更高效、可扩展的模拟与控制框架。

Abstract: World models promise a paradigm shift in robotics, where an agent learns the underlying physics of its environment once to enable efficient planning and behavior learning. However, current world models are often hardware-locked specialists: a model trained on a Boston Dynamics Spot robot fails catastrophically on a Unitree Go1 due to the mismatch in kinematic and dynamic properties, as the model overfits to specific embodiment constraints rather than capturing the universal locomotion dynamics. Consequently, a slight change in actuator dynamics or limb length necessitates training a new model from scratch. In this work, we take a step towards a framework for training a generalizable Quadrupedal World Model (QWM) that disentangles environmental dynamics from robot morphology. We address the limitations of implicit system identification, where treating static physical properties (like mass or limb length) as latent variables to be inferred from motion history creates an adaptation lag that can compromise zero-shot safety and efficiency. Instead, we explicitly condition the generative dynamics on the robot's engineering specifications. By integrating a physical morphology encoder and a reward normalizer, we enable the model to serve as a neural simulator capable of generalizing across morphologies. This capability unlocks zero-shot control across a range of embodiments. We introduce, for the first time, a world model that enables zero-shot generalization to new morphologies for locomotion. While we carefully study the limitations of our method, QWM operates as a distribution-bounded interpolator within the quadrupedal morphology family rather than a universal physics engine, this work represents a significant step toward morphology-conditioned world models for legged locomotion.

</details>


### [146] [One Interface, Many Robots: Unified Real-Time Low-Level Motion Planning for Collaborative Arms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08787)
*Yue Feng,Weicheng Huang,I-Ming Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于协作机械臂实时底层运动规划的通用接口，通过WOS中间件实现跨异构硬件平台的高可移植性，并采用多项式插值与二次规划生成平滑轨迹，在多种实验场景中验证了方法的可靠性与灵活性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了提升不同硬件平台上协作机械臂运动规划的通用性和可移植性，解决现有系统缺乏统一实时控制接口的问题。

Method: 基于WinGs Operating Studio（WOS）中间件，设计了一个轻量且灵活的实时末端轨迹控制接口，结合n次多项式插值器与二次规划求解器，生成连续可微、精确控制位置、速度和加速度的轨迹。

Result: 在三个实验中验证了该方法的有效性：离线绘制几何图形、低频重规划下抓取移动物体、以及遥操作下的灵巧操控任务，均表现出良好的实时性、可靠性和适应性。

Conclusion: 所提出的接口具有良好的通用性与实用性，能够支持多种应用场景下的实时运动控制，为异构机器人系统的集成与协作提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: This paper proposes a common interface for real-time low-level motion planning of collaborative robotic arms, aimed at enabling broader applicability and improved portability across heterogeneous hardware platforms. In previous work, we introduced WinGs Operating Studio (WOS), a middleware solution that abstracts diverse robotic components into uniform software resources and provides a broad suite of language-agnostic APIs. This paper specifically focuses on its minimal yet flexible interface for real-time end-effector trajectory control. By employing an n-degree polynomial interpolator in conjunction with a quadratic programming solver, the proposed method generates smooth, continuously differentiable trajectories with precise position, velocity, and acceleration profiles. We validate our approach in three distinct scenarios. First, in an offline demonstration, a collaborative arm accurately draws various geometric shapes on paper. Second, in an interruptible, low-frequency re-planning setting, a robotic manipulator grasps a dynamic object placed on a moving mobile robot. Finally, we conducted a teleoperation experiment in which one robotic arm controlled another to perform a series of dexterous manipulations, confirming the proposed method's reliability, versatility, and ease of use.

</details>


### [147] [Simulation of Adaptive Running with Flexible Sports Prosthesis using Reinforcement Learning of Hybrid-link System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08882)
*Yuta Shimane,Ko Yamamoto*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种基于强化学习的自适应跑步运动仿真方法，用于分析带有叶簧型运动假肢的单侧经胫截肢者在不同虚拟假肢刚度条件下的全身动力学运动，并通过模拟评估代谢运输成本，展示了该方法在虚拟条件下进行个性化假肢设计与分析的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的运动假肢设计和选择依赖于试错法，缺乏对人机交互中人体运动与假肢变形耦合效应的全面分析。因此，需要一种能够整合假肢柔顺性并实现个性化运动仿真的方法，以提升假肢的设计效率和适配精度。

Method: 采用基于混合链系统的全身体动力学分析框架，结合分段恒定应变模型描述叶簧型假肢的柔性变形；利用强化学习方法生成单侧经胫截肢者的跑步运动，融合动作捕捉数据的模仿学习与精确的假肢动力学计算。

Result: 成功模拟了不同虚拟假肢刚度下的跑步运动，结果显示假肢刚度变化显著影响代谢运输成本和跑步表现，验证了该仿真方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 所提出的强化学习结合混合链系统的仿真方法能够在虚拟环境中有效分析截肢者的运动表现，为个性化运动假肢的设计与优化提供了可行的技术路径。

Abstract: This study proposes a reinforcement learning-based adaptive running motion simulation for a unilateral transtibial amputee with the flexibility of a leaf-spring-type sports prosthesis using hybrid-link system. The design and selection of sports prostheses often rely on trial and error. A comprehensive whole-body dynamics analysis that considers the interaction between human motion and prosthetic deformation could provide valuable insights for user-specific design and selection. The hybrid-link system facilitates whole-body dynamics analysis by incorporating the Piece-wise Constant Strain model to represent the flexible deformation of the prosthesis. Based on this system, the simulation methodology generates whole-body dynamic motions of a unilateral transtibial amputee through a reinforcement learning-based approach, which combines imitation learning from motion capture data with accurate prosthetic dynamics computation. We simulated running motions under different virtual prosthetic stiffness conditions and analyzed the metabolic cost of transport obtained from the simulations, suggesting that variations in stiffness influence running performance. Our findings demonstrate the potential of this approach for simulation and analysis under virtual conditions that differ from real conditions.

</details>


### [148] [HTNav: A Hybrid Navigation Framework with Tiered Structure for Urban Aerial Vision-and-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08883)
*Chengjie Fan,Cong Pan,Zijian Liu,Ningzhong Liu,Jie Qin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为HTNav的混合模仿学习与强化学习框架，用于解决复杂城市环境中航拍视觉-语言导航（aerial VLN）任务中的泛化性、长距离路径规划和空间连续性理解不足的问题，在CityNav基准上实现了最先进的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有航拍视觉-语言导航方法在复杂城市环境中面临泛化能力弱、长距离路径规划性能差以及对空间连续性理解不足等挑战，亟需更鲁棒的导航框架。

Method: 提出HTNav框架，结合模仿学习（IL）与强化学习（RL），采用分阶段训练机制，并引入分层决策机制以实现宏观路径规划与细粒度动作控制的协同；同时设计地图表示学习模块以增强对开放域空间连续性的理解。

Result: 在CityNav基准测试中，HTNav在所有场景级别和任务难度下均达到最先进水平，显著提升了复杂城市环境中的导航精度与鲁棒性。

Conclusion: HTNav通过融合IL与RL及分层协作机制，有效提升了航拍VLN在真实城市环境中的导航性能，具有良好的应用前景。

Abstract: Inspired by the general Vision-and-Language Navigation (VLN) task, aerial VLN has attracted widespread attention, owing to its significant practical value in applications such as logistics delivery and urban inspection. However, existing methods face several challenges in complex urban environments, including insufficient generalization to unseen scenes, suboptimal performance in long-range path planning, and inadequate understanding of spatial continuity. To address these challenges, we propose HTNav, a new collaborative navigation framework that integrates Imitation Learning (IL) and Reinforcement Learning (RL) within a hybrid IL-RL framework. This framework adopts a staged training mechanism to ensure the stability of the basic navigation strategy while enhancing its environmental exploration capability. By integrating a tiered decision-making mechanism, it achieves collaborative interaction between macro-level path planning and fine-grained action control. Furthermore, a map representation learning module is introduced to deepen its understanding of spatial continuity in open domains. On the CityNav benchmark, our method achieves state-of-the-art performance across all scene levels and task difficulties. Experimental results demonstrate that this framework significantly improves navigation precision and robustness in complex urban environments.

</details>


### [149] [AssemLM: Spatial Reasoning Multimodal Large Language Models for Robotic Assembly](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08983)
*Zhi Jing,Jinbin Qiao,Ouyang Lu,Jicong Ao,Shuang Qiu,Yu-Gang Jiang,Chenjia Bai*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出AssemLM，一种面向机器人装配的三维空间多模态大语言模型，结合装配手册、点云和文本指令进行6D装配位姿推理，并构建大规模数据集AssemBench以推动具身智能中的3D空间推理研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉-语言模型依赖粗略的2D感知，缺乏对3D几何结构的精确理解，难以满足机器人精细装配任务中对空间推理的需求。

Method: 提出AssemLM，采用专用点云编码器提取细粒度几何与旋转特征，并将其融入多模态语言模型；结合装配手册、点云和文本指令实现6D位姿预测。同时构建包含90万多个样本的大规模基准AssemBench，支持精确的3D空间推理评估。

Result: 实验表明AssemLM在多种装配场景下的6D位姿推理任务中达到SOTA性能，且在真实机器人上实现了细粒度、多步骤的装配操作。

Conclusion: AssemLM通过融合精细3D感知与高层语义推理，显著提升了机器人装配中的空间理解能力，验证了其在实际应用中的有效性与潜力。

Abstract: Spatial reasoning is a fundamental capability for embodied intelligence, especially for fine-grained manipulation tasks such as robotic assembly. While recent vision-language models (VLMs) exhibit preliminary spatial awareness, they largely rely on coarse 2D perception and lack the ability to perform accurate reasoning over 3D geometry, which is crucial for precise assembly operations. To address this limitation, we propose AssemLM, a spatial multimodal large language model tailored for robotic assembly. AssemLM integrates assembly manuals, point clouds, and textual instructions to reason about and predict task-critical 6D assembly poses, enabling explicit geometric understanding throughout the assembly process. To effectively bridge raw 3D perception and high-level reasoning, we adopt a specialized point cloud encoder to capture fine-grained geometric and rotational features, which are then integrated into the multimodal language model to support accurate 3D spatial reasoning for assembly tasks. In addition, we construct AssemBench, a large-scale dataset and benchmark for assembly-oriented spatial reasoning, comprising over 900K multimodal samples with precise 6D pose annotations. AssemBench extends spatial reasoning evaluation beyond 2D and grounding tasks into full 3D geometric inference, filling a critical gap in existing embodied AI benchmarks. Extensive experiments demonstrate that AssemLM achieves state-of-the-art performance in 6D pose reasoning across diverse assembly scenarios. Furthermore, real-robot evaluations show that our model can support fine-grained and multi-step assembly execution in real-world settings, demonstrating its potential for robotic assembly applications.

</details>


### [150] [Towards Lifelong Aerial Autonomy: Geometric Memory Management for Continual Visual Place Recognition in Dynamic Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09038)
*Xingyu Shao,Zhiqiang Yan,Liangzheng Sun,Mengfan He,Chao Chen,Jinhui Zhang,Chunyu Li,Ziyang Meng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向空中视觉位置识别的异构记忆框架，以解决在持续变化环境下的灾难性遗忘问题。通过将地理知识解耦为静态卫星锚点和动态经验回放缓冲区，并采用基于特征多样性的缓冲区选择策略，显著提升了空间泛化能力和知识保留效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有持续学习方法在处理空中视觉位置识别时面临灾难性遗忘问题，尤其是在地理特征具有高度类内变异的情况下表现不佳。因此需要一种能够适应任务序列变化且满足机载存储限制的新方法。

Method: 将航空VPR建模为基于任务的领域增量学习问题，提出“学习-丢弃”框架，利用静态卫星锚点保留全局几何先验，结合动态经验回放缓冲区保存领域特异性特征，并设计了基于样本难度或特征空间多样性的空间约束分配策略。

Result: 实验表明，所提方法在知识保留上比随机基线高出7.8%，且在不同随机化序列中表现出顺序无关的鲁棒性；基于多样性驱动的缓冲区选择优于基于样本难度的方法。

Conclusion: 维持结构化的特征覆盖比单纯关注样本难度更能有效缓解长期空中自主中的灾难性遗忘问题，最大化结构多样性有助于实现更好的可塑性-稳定性平衡。

Abstract: Robust geo-localization in changing environmental conditions is critical for long-term aerial autonomy. While visual place recognition (VPR) models perform well when airborne views match the training domain, adapting them to shifting distributions during sequential missions triggers catastrophic forgetting. Existing continual learning (CL) methods often fail here because geographic features exhibit severe intra-class variations. In this work, we formulate aerial VPR as a mission-based domain-incremental learning (DIL) problem and propose a novel heterogeneous memory framework. To respect strict onboard storage constraints, our "Learn-and-Dispose" pipeline decouples geographic knowledge into static satellite anchors (preserving global geometric priors) and a dynamic experience replay buffer (retaining domain-specific features). We introduce a spatially-constrained allocation strategy that optimizes buffer selection based on sample difficulty or feature space diversity. To facilitate systematic assessment, we provide three evaluation criteria and a comprehensive benchmark derived from 21 diverse mission sequences. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our architecture significantly boosts spatial generalization; our diversity-driven buffer selection outperforms the random baseline by 7.8% in knowledge retention. Unlike class-mean preservation methods that fail in unstructured environments, maximizing structural diversity achieves a superior plasticity-stability balance and ensures order-agnostic robustness across randomized sequences. These results prove that maintaining structural feature coverage is more critical than sample difficulty for resolving catastrophic forgetting in lifelong aerial autonomy.

</details>


### [151] [On the Terminology and Geometric Aspects of Redundant Parallel Manipulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09156)
*Andreas Mueller*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对并联运动学机器（PKM）的统一术语体系，重点研究了构型空间（c-space）在运动学和驱动冗余分类中的核心作用，并通过非线性控制系统模型引入“驱动度”（DOA）概念，将PKM分为全驱动与欠驱动类型，从而实现对驱动冗余的系统性分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文献对并联机构中的驱动冗余缺乏一致定义和分类方法，且构型空间的作用未被充分强调，亟需建立统一的理论框架以明确冗余类型及其几何意义。

Method: 引入以构型空间为核心的运动学模型，区分c-space、输出与输入奇异性；构建在c-space上运行的非线性控制系统，定义‘驱动度’（DOA）为独立控制向量场的数量，并将其与自由度（DOF）对比进行分类。

Result: 明确了构型空间在分析冗余与奇异性中的关键几何作用；证明了冗余驱动可避免输入奇异性；提出了基于DOA与DOF关系的PKM驱动冗余分类方法。

Conclusion: 通过构型空间建模和驱动度概念，建立了适用于一般冗余PKM的一致性术语与分类体系，为并联机构的设计与控制提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Parallel kinematics machines (PKM) can exhibit kinematic as well as actuation redundancy. While the meaning of kinematic redundancy has been clarified already for serial manipulators, actuation redundancy, that is only possible in PKM, is differently classified in the literature. In this paper a consistent terminology for general redundant PKM is proposed. A kinematic model is introduced with the configuration space (c-space) as central part. The notion of kinematic redundancy is recalled for PKM. C-space, output, and input singularities are distinguished. The significance of the c-space geometry is emphasized, and it is pointed out geometrically that input singularities can be avoided by redundant actuation schemes. In order to distinguish different actuation schemes of PKM a non-linear control system is introduced whose dynamics evolves on the c-space. The degree of actuation (DOA) is introduced as the number of independent control vector fields, and PKM are classified as full-actuated and underactuated. Relating this DOA to the degree of freedom (DOF) allows to classify the actuation redundancy.

</details>


### [152] [Soft Electroadhesive Feet for Micro Aerial Robots Perching on Smooth and Curved Surfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09270)
*Chen Liu,Sonu Feroz,Ketao Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种用于微型飞行机器人在光滑表面停靠的柔性可拉伸电粘附垫的高效制造流程，并通过实验验证了其在不同条件下的剪切和法向粘附性能，成功实现了Crazyflie四旋翼无人机在平面和曲面的可重复附着与快速脱离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电粘附（EA）具有电控开关粘附能力，是微型飞行机器人在光滑表面停靠的有前途的方法，但目前柔软可拉伸EA垫的实际应用仍有限，因此需要开发更实用的软性EA装置以提升MAV的停靠可靠性。

Method: 设计并实现一种带有正弦波和同心圆电极的多尺寸软性可拉伸电粘附垫；利用Instron实验系统在0 kV和4.8 kV条件下对比测试其法向与剪切粘附力；将电粘附足集成到Crazyflie四旋翼上进行实际停靠演示。

Result: 剪切粘附力为主导，部分接触下可达约3 N，而法向粘附力较小且受基底材料影响显著；Crazyflie原型可在光滑塑料表面（包括曲面）实现可重复附着，并在断电后迅速脱离。

Conclusion: 柔性电粘附足具备轻量化、高可靠性的优势，展现出在微型飞行器停靠应用中的巨大潜力。

Abstract: Electroadhesion (EA) provides electrically switchable adhesion and is a promising mechanism for perching micro aerial robots on smooth surfaces. However, practical implementations of soft and stretchable EA pads for aerial perching remain limited. This work presents (i) an efficient workflow for fabricating soft, stretchable electroadhesive pads with sinusoidal wave and concentric-circle electrodes in multiple sizes, (ii) a controlled experimental comparison of normal and shear adhesion under inactive (0 kV) and active (4.8 kV) conditions using an Instron-based setup, and (iii) a perching demonstration using a Crazyflie quadrotor equipped with electroadhesive feet on flat and curved substrates. Experimental results show that shear adhesion dominates, reaching forces on the order of 3 N with partial pad contact, while normal adhesion is comparatively small and strongly dependent on substrate properties. The Crazyflie prototype demonstrates repeatable attachment on smooth plastic surfaces, including curved geometries, as well as rapid detachment when the voltage is removed. These results highlight the potential of soft electroadhesive feet for lightweight and reliable perching in micro aerial vehicles (MAVs).

</details>


### [153] [Characterizing Lidar Range-Measurement Ambiguity due to Multiple Returns](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09282)
*Jason H. Rife,Yifan Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文分析了激光雷达在道路环境中的定位性能，特别是当激光束路径中存在多个散射表面时可能出现的概率性返回现象，并提出了评估这种多返回情况对定位影响的定性方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于激光雷达在自动驾驶车辆定位中的重要性，但其在复杂道路环境下的性能尚未完全理解，尤其是当激光束遇到多个反射面时可能产生概率性测量的问题，因此需要深入研究。

Method: 通过分析两种不同机械旋转激光雷达设备在静止基座下观测到的激光路径数据集，生成代表性累积分布函数（CDF），并提出一种定性方法来评估概率性多返回对定位的影响。

Result: 发现了激光雷达在特定路径上确实存在概率性返回的现象，并提供了描述这些情况的累积分布函数，同时提出了评估其对定位系统影响的方法。

Conclusion: 激光雷达在实际道路环境中可能不会总是沿给定路径识别出唯一表面，这一发现对现有定位算法提出了挑战，并为改进激光雷达定位系统的鲁棒性提供了方向。

Abstract: Reliable position and attitude sensing is critical for highly automated vehicles that operate on conventional roadways. Lidar sensors are increasingly incorporated into pose-estimation systems. Despite its great utility, lidar is a complex sensor, and its performance in roadway environments is not yet well understood. For instance, it is often assumed in lidar-localization algorithms that a lidar will always identify a unique surface along a given raypath. However, this assumption is not always true, as ample prior evidence exists to suggest that lidar units may generate measurements probabilistically when more than one scattering surface appears within the lidar's conical beam. In this paper, we analyze lidar datasets to characterize cases with probabilistic returns along particular raypaths. Our contribution is to present representative cumulative distribution functions (CDFs) for raypaths observed by two different mechanically rotating lidar units with stationary bases. In subsequent discussion, we outline a qualitative methodology to assess the effect of probabilistic multi-return cases on lidar-based localization.

</details>


### [154] [A Benchmark of Dexterity for Anthropomorphic Robotic Hands](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09294)
*Davide Liconti,Yuning Zhou,Yasunori Toshimitsu,Ronan Hinchet,Robert K. Katzschmann*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了POMDAR，一个基于任务表现的机器人手部灵巧性综合评测基准，旨在通过标准化和可量化的指标实现对仿人机器人手设计的客观、可重复评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于目前对机器人手部灵巧性的定义和评估标准不统一，导致不同系统之间难以进行有意义的比较，因此需要一种基于性能、可测量且统一的灵巧性定义。
