Method: 基于人类运动控制领域的成熟分类体系，构建了一个包含四种操作配置（垂直与水平操作、连续旋转、纯抓取）的任务集，并在真实环境和仿真中实现；引入机械辅助结构以限制动作自由度、防止补偿策略并确保测量清晰；定义了结合任务正确率和执行速度的量化评分指标，将灵巧性衡量为操作吞吐量。

Result: 实现了可在真实与仿真环境中运行的标准化评测平台，提供了客观、可复现且可解释的评估结果；所有CAD模型、仿真文件和评测视频均已开源。

Conclusion: POMDAR为仿人机器人手提供了一个开放、标准化且基于分类体系的灵巧性评测基准，有助于推动灵巧操作平台的系统性发展。

Abstract: Dexterity is a central yet ambiguously defined concept in the design and evaluation of anthropomorphic robotic hands. In practice, the term is often used inconsistently, with different systems evaluated under disparate criteria, making meaningful comparisons across designs difficult. This highlights the need for a unified, performance-based definition of dexterity grounded in measurable outcomes rather than proxy metrics. In this work, we introduce POMDAR, a comprehensive dexterity benchmark that formalizes dexterity as task performance across a structured set of manipulation and grasping motions. The benchmark was systematically derived from established taxonomies in human motor control. It is implemented in both real-world and simulation and includes four manipulation configurations: vertical and horizontal configurations, continuous rotation, and pure grasping. The task designs contain mechanical scaffolding to constrain task motion, suppress compensatory strategies, and enable metrics to be measured unambiguously. We define a quantitative scoring metric combining task correctness and execution speed, effectively measuring dexterity as throughput. This enables objective, reproducible, and interpretable evaluation across different hand designs. POMDAR provides an open-source, standardized, and taxonomy-grounded benchmark for consistent comparison and evaluation of anthropomorphic robot hands to facilitate a systematic advancement of dexterous manipulation platforms. CAD, simulation files, and evaluation videos are publicly available at https://srl-ethz.github.io/POMDAR/.

</details>


### [155] [Online Intention Prediction via Control-Informed Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09303)
*Tianyu Zhou,Zihao Liang,Zehui Lu,Shaoshuai Mou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种在线意图预测框架，用于实时估计自主系统的动态目标状态，适用于存在未知参数的系统动力学或目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了在时间变化和系统不确定性的情况下实现对自主系统意图的准确实时预测。

Method: 将问题建模为逆最优控制/逆强化学习任务，利用滑动时间窗策略忽略过时信息，并通过在线控制引导的学习实现高效梯度计算和参数更新。

Result: 仿真和四旋翼无人机硬件实验表明，该方法在不同噪声水平和复杂环境中均能实现精确且自适应的意图预测。

Conclusion: 所提出的框架能够有效处理动态变化和未知参数，实现实时、鲁棒的意图预测。

Abstract: This paper presents an online intention prediction framework for estimating the goal state of autonomous systems in real time, even when intention is time-varying, and system dynamics or objectives include unknown parameters. The problem is formulated as an inverse optimal control / inverse reinforcement learning task, with the intention treated as a parameter in the objective. A shifting horizon strategy discounts outdated information, while online control-informed learning enables efficient gradient computation and online parameter updates. Simulations under varying noise levels and hardware experiments on a quadrotor drone demonstrate that the proposed approach achieves accurate, adaptive intention prediction in complex environments.

</details>


### [156] [Robust Adaptive Backstepping Impedance Control of Robots in Unknown Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09323)
*Reza Nazmara,Alap Kshirsagar,Jan Peters,A. Pedro Aguiar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种鲁棒自适应反步阻抗控制（RABIC）策略，用于机器人在接触丰富且不确定环境中运行，具有良好的稳定性和实验效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了提升机器人在接触丰富和不确定性环境中的操作性能，克服外部干扰和未建模动态的影响，并避免依赖精确的动力学参数。

Method: 采用基于反步法的自适应阻抗控制内环，结合泰勒级数估计器估计系统动态，自适应估计器估计外力上界，实现无需动力学参数的鲁棒控制。

Result: 仿真和真实Franka Emika Panda机器人实验表明，该方法在轨迹跟踪和力监控方面表现良好，相比PD控制更安全。

Conclusion: RABIC框架为移动与固定串联机械臂的自适应及学习型阻抗控制研究提供了坚实基础。

Abstract: This paper presents a Robust Adaptive Backstepping Impedance Control (RABIC) strategy for robots operating in contact-rich and uncertain environments. The proposed control strategy considers the complete coupled dynamics of the system and explicitly accounts for key sources of uncertainty, including external disturbances and unmodeled dynamics, while not requiring the robot's dynamic parameters in implementation. We propose a backstepping-based adaptive impedance control scheme for the inner loop to track the reference impedance model. To handle uncertainties, we employ a Taylor series-based estimator for system dynamics and an adaptive estimator for determining the upper bound of external forces. Stability analysis demonstrates the semi-global practical finite-time stability of the overall system. To demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed method, a simulated mobile manipulator scenario and experimental evaluations on a real Franka Emika Panda robot were conducted. The proposed approach exhibits safer performance compared to PD control while ensuring trajectory tracking and force monitoring. Overall, the RABIC framework provides a solid basis for future research on adaptive and learning-based impedance control for coupled mobile and fixed serially linked manipulators.

</details>


### [157] [Multimodal Anomaly Detection for Human-Robot Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09326)
*Guilherme Ribeiro,Iordanis Antypas,Leonardo Bizzaro,João Bimbo,Nuno Cruz Garcia*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于人机协作中异常检测的框架MADRI，该框架将视频流转换为语义特征向量，并结合机器人内部传感器数据和场景图进行多模态重构，有效提升了异常检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在人机交互中，确保安全性和可靠性需要及时检测可能导致系统故障或不安全行为的意外事件。现有的基于重建的模型多集中于原始数据，而直接在特征向量上操作的方法研究较少。

Method: 提出MADRI框架：首先将视频流转化为语义特征向量，然后在此基础上进行基于重构的异常检测；同时融合机器人内部传感器读数和场景图，实现对外部视觉环境异常和内部机器人故障的联合检测。

Result: 实验结果表明，仅使用基于视觉的特征向量即可有效检测异常，而加入其他模态信息后检测性能进一步提升。

Conclusion: 多模态特征重构有助于提升人机协作中异常检测的鲁棒性，验证了融合视觉、内部传感与结构化场景信息的有效性。

Abstract: Ensuring safety and reliability in human-robot interaction (HRI) requires the timely detection of unexpected events that could lead to system failures or unsafe behaviours. Anomaly detection thus plays a critical role in enabling robots to recognize and respond to deviations from normal operation during collaborative tasks. While reconstruction models have been actively explored in HRI, approaches that operate directly on feature vectors remain largely unexplored. In this work, we propose MADRI, a framework that first transforms video streams into semantically meaningful feature vectors before performing reconstruction-based anomaly detection. Additionally, we augment these visual feature vectors with the robot's internal sensors' readings and a Scene Graph, enabling the model to capture both external anomalies in the visual environment and internal failures within the robot itself. To evaluate our approach, we collected a custom dataset consisting of a simple pick-and-place robotic task under normal and anomalous conditions. Experimental results demonstrate that reconstruction on vision-based feature vectors alone is effective for detecting anomalies, while incorporating other modalities further improves detection performance, highlighting the benefits of multimodal feature reconstruction for robust anomaly detection in human-robot collaboration.

</details>


### [158] [VAG: Dual-Stream Video-Action Generation for Embodied Data Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09330)
*Xiaolei Lang,Yang Wang,Yukun Zhou,Chaojun Ni,Kerui Li,Jiagang Zhu,Tianze Liu,Jiajun Lv,Xingxing Zuo,Yun Ye,Guan Huang,Xiaofeng Wang,Zheng Zhu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为VAG的统一双流框架，基于流匹配技术，联合生成视频和动作，提升了跨模态一致性，适用于机器人策略学习中的合成数据生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的世界模型难以直接用于策略学习，因缺乏配对的动作轨迹；现有方法在视频-动作对齐或生成效率方面存在不足。

Method: 提出VAG框架，采用基于流匹配的双流结构，通过同步去噪和自适应3D池化机制，在视觉和语言条件下联合生成视频与动作。

Result: 在模拟和真实场景中，VAG能生成高质量且对齐的视频-动作对，支持可执行轨迹回放，并提供有效的合成预训练数据。

Conclusion: VAG是一种有潜力的具身数据合成世界-动作模型，能够提升下游策略的泛化能力。

Abstract: Recent advances in robot foundation models trained on large-scale human teleoperation data have enabled robots to perform increasingly complex real-world tasks. However, scaling these systems remains difficult because collecting task-specific demonstrations is expensive and labor-intensive. Synthetic data, especially generated videos, offer a promising direction, but existing World Models (WMs) are not directly suitable for policy learning since they do not provide paired action trajectories. World-Action (WA) models partially address this by predicting actions with visual outputs, yet often lack strong video-action alignment, while two-stage pipelines that generate video first and then infer actions introduce inefficiency and error accumulation. To address these limitations, we propose VAG, a unified flow-matching-based dual-stream framework that jointly generates video and action under visual and language conditioning. By synchronizing denoising in both branches and using an adaptive 3D pooling mechanism to transfer compact global video context to the action branch, VAG improves cross-modal consistency during generation. Across both simulated and real-world settings, VAG produces aligned video-action pairs with competitive prediction quality, supports executable trajectory replay, and provides useful synthetic pretraining data that improves downstream policy generalization, indicating its potential as a practical world-action model for embodied data synthesis.

</details>


### [159] [Musculoskeletal Motion Imitation for Learning Personalized Exoskeleton Control Policy in Impaired Gait](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09431)
*Itak Choi,Ilseung Park,Eni Halilaj,Inseung Kang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种设备无关的框架，结合生理上合理的肌肉骨骼模拟与强化学习，实现可扩展的个性化外骨骼辅助控制，适用于健康和临床人群。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 设计通用的下肢外骨骼控制策略受限于大量数据收集或迭代优化，限制了在临床人群中的应用。

Method: 结合生理上合理的肌肉骨骼模拟与强化学习，构建设备无关的个性化外骨骼控制框架。

Result: 控制策略生成符合生理规律的运动动态，并捕捉肌肉缺陷下的代偿策略；在无需任务特定调优的情况下，产生的辅助扭矩与实验验证结果一致，且降低代谢成本；对步态障碍模型提供不对称、缺陷特异性的辅助，改善能量效率和双侧运动对称性。

Conclusion: 基于强化学习的生理合理肌肉骨骼模拟可作为跨健康与临床人群的个性化外骨骼控制的可扩展基础，无需大量实际试验。

Abstract: Designing generalizable control policies for lower-limb exoskeletons remains fundamentally constrained by exhaustive data collection or iterative optimization procedures, which limit accessibility to clinical populations. To address this challenge, we introduce a device-agnostic framework that combines physiologically plausible musculoskeletal simulation with reinforcement learning to enable scalable personalized exoskeleton assistance for both able-bodied and clinical populations. Our control policies not only generate physiologically plausible locomotion dynamics but also capture clinically observed compensatory strategies under targeted muscular deficits, providing a unified computational model of both healthy and pathological gait. Without task-specific tuning, the resulting exoskeleton control policies produce assistive torque profiles at the hip and ankle that align with state-of-the-art profiles validated in human experiments, while consistently reducing metabolic cost across walking speeds. For simulated impaired-gait models, the learned control policies yield asymmetric, deficit-specific exoskeleton assistance that improves both energetic efficiency and bilateral kinematic symmetry without explicit prescription of the target gait pattern. These results demonstrate that physiologically plausible musculoskeletal simulation via reinforcement learning can serve as a scalable foundation for personalized exoskeleton control across both able-bodied and clinical populations, eliminating the need for extensive physical trials.

</details>


### [160] [Adaptor: Advancing Assistive Teleoperation with Few-Shot Learning and Cross-Operator Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09462)
*Yu Liu,Yihang Yin,Tianlv Huang,Fei Yan,Yuan Xu,Weinan Hong,Wei Han,Yue Cao,Xiangyu Chen,Zipei Fan,Xuan Song*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为Adaptor的少样本框架，用于实现鲁棒的跨操作者意图识别，通过预处理和策略学习两个阶段提升辅助遥操作中的意图识别稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于不同操作者的习惯和专业知识差异，导致轨迹分布高度异质，从而削弱了意图识别的稳定性。

Method: Adaptor框架包括两个阶段：(i) 预处理阶段，通过噪声注入合成轨迹扰动以建模意图不确定性，并进行几何感知的关键帧提取；(ii) 策略学习阶段，利用意图专家编码处理后的轨迹，并将其与预训练的视觉-语言模型上下文融合，以条件化动作专家生成动作。

Result: 在真实世界和模拟基准上的实验表明，Adaptor在成功率和效率上优于基线方法，并且在不同专业知识的操作者之间表现出低方差，展示了强大的跨操作者泛化能力。

Conclusion: Adaptor能够有效桥接域差距，实现稳健的跨操作者意图识别，具有较高的应用潜力。

Abstract: Assistive teleoperation enhances efficiency via shared control, yet inter-operator variability, stemming from diverse habits and expertise, induces highly heterogeneous trajectory distributions that undermine intent recognition stability. We present Adaptor, a few-shot framework for robust cross-operator intent recognition. The Adaptor bridges the domain gap through two stages: (i) preprocessing, which models intent uncertainty by synthesizing trajectory perturbations via noise injection and performs geometry-aware keyframe extraction; and (ii) policy learning, which encodes the processed trajectories with an Intention Expert and fuses them with the pre-trained vision-language model context to condition an Action Expert for action generation. Experiments on real-world and simulated benchmarks demonstrate that Adaptor achieves state-of-the-art performance, improving success rates and efficiency over baselines. Moreover, the method exhibits low variance across operators with varying expertise, demonstrating robust cross-operator generalization.

</details>


### [161] [SafeMind: A Risk-Aware Differentiable Control Framework for Adaptive and Safe Quadruped Locomotion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09474)
*Zukun Zhang,Kai Shu,Mingqiao Mo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SafeMind是一种基于学习的四足机器人安全控制框架，结合概率控制屏障函数与语义理解，能在不确定性环境下提供形式化安全保证，显著减少安全违规和能耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于学习的四足控制器在模型不确定、感知噪声和非结构化接触条件下缺乏形式化安全保证。

Method: 提出SafeMind，采用可微分随机安全控制框架，融合概率控制屏障函数、语义上下文理解和元自适应风险校准；通过方差感知的屏障约束建模认知和随机不确定性，并设计语义到约束编码器与元自适应学习器调节安全裕度和风险敏感性。

Result: 理论证明了在随机动力学下的概率前向不变性、可行性与稳定性；在Unitree A1和ANYmal C上以200Hz实现部署，在12种地形、动态障碍、形态扰动和语义任务中验证，相比现有CBF、MPC和混合RL基线，安全违规减少3-10倍，能耗降低10-15%。

Conclusion: SafeMind在保持实时控制性能的同时，显著提升了复杂环境下的安全性与能效，为学习型控制器提供了可靠的安全保障机制。

Abstract: Learning-based quadruped controllers achieve impressive agility but typically lack formal safety guarantees under model uncertainty, perception noise, and unstructured contact conditions. We introduce SafeMind, a differentiable stochastic safety-control framework that unifies probabilistic Control Barrier Functions with semantic context understanding and meta-adaptive risk calibration. SafeMind explicitly models epistemic and aleatoric uncertainty through a variance-aware barrier constraint embedded in a differentiable quadratic program, thereby preserving gradient flow for end-to-end training. A semantics-to-constraint encoder modulates safety margins using perceptual or language cues, while a meta-adaptive learner continuously adjusts risk sensitivity across environments. We provide theoretical conditions for probabilistic forward invariance, feasibility, and stability under stochastic dynamics. SafeMind is deployed on Unitree A1 and ANYmal C at 200~Hz and validated across 12 terrain types, dynamic obstacles, morphology perturbations, and semantically defined tasks. Experiments show that SafeMind reduces safety violations by 3--10x and energy consumption by 10--15% relative to state-of-the-art CBF, MPC, and hybrid RL baselines, while maintaining real-time control performance.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [162] [RAMP: Hybrid DRL for Online Learning of Numeric Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08685)
*Yarin Benyamin,Argaman Mordoch,Shahaf S. Shperberg,Roni Stern*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出RAMP策略，通过与环境交互在线学习数值规划动作模型，并结合深度强化学习与规划，在标准IPC数值领域中显著优于PPO算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有从观测中学习动作模型的方法多为离线且依赖专家轨迹，难以应用于需要在线学习的实际场景，尤其是在数值规划领域缺乏有效的在线学习方法。

Method: 提出RAMP（Reinforcement learning, Action Model learning, and Planning）框架，结合深度强化学习（DRL）策略训练、从交互数据中学习数值动作模型，并利用该模型进行规划；同时构建Numeric PDDLGym框架以支持将数值规划问题转化为Gym环境。

Result: 在标准IPC数值规划域上的实验表明，RAMP在求解成功率和计划质量上显著优于PPO算法。

Conclusion: RAMP实现了在线学习可解释的数值动作模型，并通过强化学习与规划的正向反馈循环提升了整体性能，展示了RL与规划协同的有效性。

Abstract: Automated planning algorithms require an action model specifying the preconditions and effects of each action, but obtaining such a model is often hard. Learning action models from observations is feasible, but existing algorithms for numeric domains are offline, requiring expert traces as input. We propose the Reinforcement learning, Action Model learning, and Planning (RAMP) strategy for learning numeric planning action models online via interactions with the environment. RAMP simultaneously trains a Deep Reinforcement Learning (DRL) policy, learns a numeric action model from past interactions, and uses that model to plan future actions when possible. These components form a positive feedback loop: the RL policy gathers data to refine the action model, while the planner generates plans to continue training the RL policy. To facilitate this integration of RL and numeric planning, we developed Numeric PDDLGym, an automated framework for converting numeric planning problems to Gym environments. Experimental results on standard IPC numeric domains show that RAMP significantly outperforms PPO, a well-known DRL algorithm, in terms of solvability and plan quality.

</details>


### [163] [Parameterized Complexity Of Representing Models Of MSO Formulas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08707)
*Petr Kučera,Petr Martinek*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文扩展了Courcelle定理，证明了在考虑树宽或路径宽时，MSO2公式的模型可用大小参数化线性的决策图（SDD或OBDD）表示，并揭示了OBDD在某些情况下无法仅由树宽参数化大小的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了将Courcelle定理推广到自由变量的MSO2公式模型表示，并连接参数复杂性与知识表示领域。

Method: 通过分析SDD和OBDD在不同图参数（如树宽和路径宽）下的大小上界，并结合Razgon（2014）的下界结果，论证OBDD的局限性。

Result: 建立了SDD在树宽下的参数化线性大小上界和OBDD在路径宽下的相应上界；同时证明存在某些有界树宽图类和MSO2公式，其OBDD大小不能仅由树宽参数化。

Conclusion: 该工作为Courcelle定理提供了新视角，表明使用适当的决策图可高效表示MSO2模型，但OBDD在树宽参数化下存在本质限制，从而加强了参数复杂性与知识表示之间的联系。

Abstract: Monadic second order logic (MSO2) plays an important role in parameterized complexity due to the Courcelle's theorem. This theorem states that the problem of checking if a given graph has a property specified by a given MSO2 formula can be solved by a parameterized linear time algorithm with respect to the treewidth of the graph and the size of the formula. We extend this result by showing that models of MSO2 formula with free variables can be represented with a decision diagram whose size is parameterized linear in the above mentioned parameter. In particular, we show a parameterized linear upper bound on the size of a sentential decision diagram (SDD) when treewidth is considered and a parameterized linear upper bound on the size of an ordered binary decision diagram (OBDD) when considering the pathwidth in the parameter. In addition, building on a lower bound on the size of OBDD by Razgon (2014), we show that there is an MSO2 formula and a class of graphs with bounded treewidth which do not admit an OBDD with the size parameterized by the treewidth. Our result offers a new perspective on the Courcelle's theorem and connects it to the area of knowledge representation.

</details>


### [164] [Artifacts as Memory Beyond the Agent Boundary](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08756)
*John D. Martin,Fraser Mince,Esra'a Saleh,Amy Pajak*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种强化学习中的新框架，形式化地描述环境如何作为智能体的记忆使用，证明特定观测（即‘人工物’）可减少历史信息表示所需的记忆量，并通过实验验证空间路径观察能降低学习高性能策略所需的记忆负担。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受认知科学中情境认知观点启发，旨在形式化环境中资源如何辅助或替代智能体内在记忆，以提升强化学习效率。

Method: 引入数学框架建模环境作为功能记忆的机制，定义‘人工物’概念并证明其可减少历史依赖的信息量，结合实验分析智能体在观察空间路径时的记忆需求变化。

Result: 实验证明，当智能体感知到空间路径等环境线索时，学习有效策略所需的记忆显著减少，且该效应通过感觉流隐式、非意图性产生。

Conclusion: 环境可通过特定观测充当外部记忆，满足已有外部记忆理论的关键性质，为未来利用环境替代内部记忆提供了理论基础与研究方向。

Abstract: The situated view of cognition holds that intelligent behavior depends not only on internal memory, but on an agent's active use of environmental resources. Here, we begin formalizing this intuition within Reinforcement Learning (RL). We introduce a mathematical framing for how the environment can functionally serve as an agent's memory, and prove that certain observations, which we call artifacts, can reduce the information needed to represent history. We corroborate our theory with experiments showing that when agents observe spatial paths, the amount of memory required to learn a performant policy is reduced. Interestingly, this effect arises unintentionally, and implicitly through the agent's sensory stream. We discuss the implications of our findings, and show they satisfy qualitative properties previously used to ground accounts of external memory. Moving forward, we anticipate further work on this subject could reveal principled ways to exploit the environment as a substitute for explicit internal memory.

</details>


### [165] [Hidden in Plain Sight: Visual-to-Symbolic Analytical Solution Inference from Field Visualizations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08863)
*Pengze Li,Jiaquan Zhang,Yunbo Long,Xinping Liu,Zhou wenjie,Encheng Su,Zihang Zeng,Jiaqi Liu,Jiyao Liu,Junchi Yu,Lihao Liu,Philip Torr,Shixiang Tang,Aoran Wang,Xi Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为ViSA-R2的新方法，用于从二维线性稳态场的可视化图像中推断出符号化的解析解。该方法结合自验证、以解为中心的思维链流程，遵循物理学家式的推理路径，并在新发布的基准数据集ViSA-Bench上表现出优于现有开源和闭源视觉语言模型的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从视觉观测中恢复物理场的解析解是AI辅助科学推理的一项基础但尚未充分探索的能力。现有方法难以准确生成具有完全实例化数值常数的可执行符号表达式。

Method: 提出ViSA-R2方法，采用基于8B参数Qwen3-VL的视觉语言模型，结合自验证、以解为中心的链式思维推理流程，依次进行结构模式识别、解族假设、参数推导与一致性验证，最终输出单个可执行的SymPy表达式。

Result: 在新构建的ViSA-Bench基准（涵盖30种线性稳态场景）上评估，ViSA-R2在数值精度、表达式结构相似性和字符级准确率方面均优于强开源基线和评估的闭源前沿视觉语言模型。

Conclusion: ViSA-R2通过模拟物理学家的推理路径，有效实现了从视觉输入到精确符号解的端到端推断，推动了AI在科学发现中的应用能力。

Abstract: Recovering analytical solutions of physical fields from visual observations is a fundamental yet underexplored capability for AI-assisted scientific reasoning. We study visual-to-symbolic analytical solution inference (ViSA) for two-dimensional linear steady-state fields: given field visualizations (and first-order derivatives) plus minimal auxiliary metadata, the model must output a single executable SymPy expression with fully instantiated numeric constants. We introduce ViSA-R2 and align it with a self-verifying, solution-centric chain-of-thought pipeline that follows a physicist-like pathway: structural pattern recognition solution-family (ansatz) hypothesis parameter derivation consistency verification. We also release ViSA-Bench, a VLM-ready synthetic benchmark covering 30 linear steady-state scenarios with verifiable analytical/symbolic annotations, and evaluate predictions by numerical accuracy, expression-structure similarity, and character-level accuracy. Using an 8B open-weight Qwen3-VL backbone, ViSA-R2 outperforms strong open-source baselines and the evaluated closed-source frontier VLMs under a standardized protocol.

</details>


### [166] [SPPO: Sequence-Level PPO for Long-Horizon Reasoning Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08865)
*Tianyi Wang,Yixia Li,Long Li,Yibiao Chen,Shaohan Huang,Yun Chen,Peng Li,Yang Liu,Guanhua Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的算法SPPO，用于在具有可验证奖励的推理任务中对齐大语言模型。该方法通过将推理过程重新定义为序列级上下文赌博机问题，并采用解耦的标量价值函数来生成低方差优势信号，从而解决了标准PPO在长链思维（CoT）场景中的不稳定性及内存成本高的问题。实验表明，SPPO显著优于标准PPO，并且性能与计算密集型的基于组的方法相当，同时具备更高的资源效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准的token-level PPO在处理长链思维（CoT）推理任务时存在时间信用分配不稳定和价值模型内存消耗过大的问题；而无评论者的方法如GRPO虽然缓解了这些问题，但因需要多次采样估计基线而导致计算开销大、训练吞吐量低。因此，需要一种兼具样本效率、训练稳定性和高吞吐量的新型对齐算法。

Method: 提出了Sequence-Level PPO (SPPO)，将推理过程建模为序列级上下文赌博机问题，使用解耦的标量价值函数计算优势函数，避免多采样需求，在保持PPO样本效率的同时实现稳定的结局导向更新。

Result: 在多个数学推理基准上的实验显示，SPPO显著优于标准PPO，在性能上与计算昂贵的基于组的方法（如GRPO）相当，同时大幅提升了训练效率和资源利用率。

Conclusion: SPPO是一种高效、稳定且可扩展的推理对齐算法，能够在不牺牲性能的前提下显著降低训练成本，为大语言模型的推理能力对齐提供了一个实用且资源友好的解决方案。

Abstract: Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO) is central to aligning Large Language Models (LLMs) in reasoning tasks with verifiable rewards. However, standard token-level PPO struggles in this setting due to the instability of temporal credit assignment over long Chain-of-Thought (CoT) horizons and the prohibitive memory cost of the value model. While critic-free alternatives like GRPO mitigate these issues, they incur significant computational overhead by requiring multiple samples for baseline estimation, severely limiting training throughput. In this paper, we introduce Sequence-Level PPO (SPPO), a scalable algorithm that harmonizes the sample efficiency of PPO with the stability of outcome-based updates. SPPO reformulates the reasoning process as a Sequence-Level Contextual Bandit problem, employing a decoupled scalar value function to derive low-variance advantage signals without multi-sampling. Extensive experiments on mathematical benchmarks demonstrate that SPPO significantly surpasses standard PPO and matches the performance of computation-heavy group-based methods, offering a resource-efficient framework for aligning reasoning LLMs.

</details>


### [167] [StaRPO: Stability-Augmented Reinforcement Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08905)
*Jinghan Zhang,Fengran Mo,Tharindu Cyril Weerasooriya,Ruimin Dai,Xiaoyan Han,Yanjie Fu,Dakuo Wang,Kunpeng Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为StaRPO的稳定性增强型强化学习框架，通过引入自相关函数（ACF）和路径效率（PE）两个轻量级指标来衡量推理过程中的局部连贯性和全局目标导向性，从而提升大语言模型在复杂推理任务中的准确性和逻辑稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有强化学习方法仅依赖最终答案正确性作为反馈信号，忽视了推理过程的内部逻辑结构，导致生成的回答虽流畅但可能存在逻辑不一致或结构混乱的问题。因此需要一种能捕捉并优化推理过程稳定性的新框架。

Method: 提出StaRPO框架，将稳定性分解为两个可计算的指标：自相关函数（ACF）用于评估步骤间的局部连贯性，路径效率（PE）用于评估推理路径的全局目标导向性；并将这两个稳定性奖励与任务奖励结合，提供更全面的过程感知反馈。

Result: 在两个骨干模型上验证了ACF和PE奖励与逻辑错误之间的相关性；在四个推理基准上的实验表明，StaRPO在最终答案准确率和逻辑稳定性方面均优于基线方法。

Conclusion: StaRPO通过显式建模推理过程的稳定性，有效提升了大语言模型在复杂推理任务中的性能，兼顾了结果正确性和推理过程的质量。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) is effective in enhancing the accuracy of large language models in complex reasoning tasks. Existing RL policy optimization frameworks rely on final-answer correctness as feedback signals and rarely capture the internal logical structure of the reasoning process. Consequently, the models would generate fluent and semantically relevant responses but logically inconsistent, structurally erratic, or redundant. To this end, we propose StaRPO, a stability-augmented reinforcement learning framework that explicitly incorporates reasoning stability into the optimization objective. Our StaRPO decomposes stability into two computable lightweight metrics: the Autocorrelation Function (ACF) to evaluate local step-to-step coherence, and Path Efficiency (PE) to evaluate global goal-directedness of the reasoning trajectory. These stability rewards are combined with task rewards to provide complementary and process-aware feedback. We validate the effectiveness of using ACF and PE rewards by showing their correlation with logic errors on two backbone models. Experiments on four reasoning benchmarks show that StaRPO consistently outperforms compared baselines and can enhance both final-answer accuracy and logical stability.

</details>


### [168] [PilotBench: A Benchmark for General Aviation Agents with Safety Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08987)
*Yalun Wu,Haotian Liu,Zhoujun Li,Boyang Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PilotBench是一个评估大语言模型在安全关键飞行轨迹和姿态预测中表现的基准，揭示了传统预测器与大语言模型之间的精度-可控性权衡，并指出大语言模型在高工作负荷阶段性能下降的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型向具身智能体发展，需要评估其在物理环境（如飞行控制）中的复杂物理推理和安全约束遵守能力。

Method: 构建包含708个真实通用航空轨迹的PilotBench基准，涵盖九个不同飞行阶段并同步34通道遥测数据，提出结合回归精度（60%）、指令遵循和安全性（40%）的Pilot-Score综合指标，对41种模型进行比较分析。

Result: 发现传统预测器在MAE上表现更好（7.01），而大语言模型在指令遵循率上达到86%-89%，但MAE为11-14；在爬升和进近等高负荷阶段，大语言模型性能显著下降，显示出其隐式物理模型的脆弱性。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了精度与可控性之间的权衡以及动态复杂性差距，建议采用结合大语言模型符号推理与专用预测器数值精度的混合架构，以推动安全约束下的具身AI发展。

Abstract: As Large Language Models (LLMs) advance toward embodied AI agents operating in physical environments, a fundamental question emerges: can models trained on text corpora reliably reason about complex physics while adhering to safety constraints? We address this through PilotBench, a benchmark evaluating LLMs on safety-critical flight trajectory and attitude prediction. Built from 708 real-world general aviation trajectories spanning nine operationally distinct flight phases with synchronized 34-channel telemetry, PilotBench systematically probes the intersection of semantic understanding and physics-governed prediction through comparative analysis of LLMs and traditional forecasters. We introduce Pilot-Score, a composite metric balancing 60% regression accuracy with 40% instruction adherence and safety compliance. Comparative evaluation across 41 models uncovers a Precision-Controllability Dichotomy: traditional forecasters achieve superior MAE of 7.01 but lack semantic reasoning capabilities, while LLMs gain controllability with 86--89% instruction-following at the cost of 11--14 MAE precision. Phase-stratified analysis further exposes a Dynamic Complexity Gap-LLM performance degrades sharply in high-workload phases such as Climb and Approach, suggesting brittle implicit physics models. These empirical discoveries motivate hybrid architectures combining LLMs' symbolic reasoning with specialized forecasters' numerical precision. PilotBench provides a rigorous foundation for advancing embodied AI in safety-constrained domains.

</details>


### [169] [SEA-Eval: A Benchmark for Evaluating Self-Evolving Agents Beyond Episodic Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08988)
*Sihang Jiang,Lipeng Ma,Zhonghua Hong,Keyi Wang,Zhiyu Lu,Shisong Chen,Jinghao Zhang,Tianjun Pan,Weijia Zhou,Jiaqing Liang,Yanghua Xiao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了自我进化智能体（SEA）的新定义，并引入了首个评估SEA特性的基准SEA-Eval，揭示了当前主流框架在长期演化性能上的瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于大语言模型的智能体受限于静态工具集和任务间遗忘，缺乏跨任务的经验积累与策略优化能力，亟需新的框架与评估手段推动其向真正自我进化的数字实体发展。

Method: 提出以数字具身和持续跨任务演变为基础的SEA形式化定义，构建包含任务流序列的SEA-Eval基准，从任务内执行可靠性与长期演化性能两个维度进行评估，通过成功率和Token消耗的变化衡量进化增益与结构稳定性。

Result: 实验发现当前先进框架在相同成功率下，Token消耗差异高达31.2倍，且演化轨迹显著不同，暴露出严重的长期演化瓶颈。

Conclusion: SEA-Eval为评估和推动智能体从任务执行者向自我进化系统转变提供了科学基础和有效工具。

Abstract: Current LLM-based agents demonstrate strong performance in episodic task execution but remain constrained by static toolsets and episodic amnesia, failing to accumulate experience or optimize strategies across task boundaries. While the Self-Evolving Agent (SEA) paradigm has been previously proposed, this paper contributes a new formal definition of SEA grounded in digital embodiment and continuous cross-task evolution, and introduces SEA-Eval, the first benchmark designed to evaluate SEA characteristics across two dimensions, intra-task execution reliability and long-term evolutionary performance. By organizing tasks into sequential streams and analyzing Success Rate and Token Consumption over time, SEA-Eval quantifies evolutionary gain and structural stability in ways that existing episodic benchmarks cannot. Empirical evaluations reveal a significant evolutionary bottleneck in current state-of-the-art frameworks, where identical success rates mask up to 31.2 times differences in token consumption and divergent evolutionary trajectories under sequential analysis. SEA-Eval provides a rigorous scientific foundation for advancing agents from mere task executors toward genuinely self-evolving digital entities.

</details>


### [170] [Advantage-Guided Diffusion for Model-Based Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09035)
*Daniele Foffano,Arvid Eriksson,David Broman,Karl H. Johansson,Alexandre Proutiere*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了AGD-MBRL，一种基于优势估计引导扩散过程的优势感知模型，用于缓解扩散世界模型在短视域下的局限性，在MuJoCo任务中显著提升了样本效率和最终回报。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散引导方法要么仅依赖策略、忽略价值信息，要么使用奖励引导，在短扩散视野下容易变得短视，难以实现长期回报优化。

Method: 提出两种优势引导方式：Sigmoid Advantage Guidance (SAG) 和 Exponential Advantage Guidance (EAG)，利用智能体的优势估计来指导扩散模型的反向过程，并结合PolyGRAD架构实现轨迹生成与策略条件动作生成的协同。

Result: 理论证明SAG/EAG可实现按状态-动作优势加权的轨迹重采样，生成的轨迹遵循更高价值的改进策略；实验表明AGD-MBRL在HalfCheetah、Hopper等MuJoCo任务上优于PolyGRAD、Diffuser式方法及PPO/TRPO等无模型基线。

Conclusion: 优势感知引导是一种简单而有效的解决方案，能够克服扩散模型MBRL中的短视问题，提升长期预测质量与控制性能。

Abstract: Model-based reinforcement learning (MBRL) with autoregressive world models suffers from compounding errors, whereas diffusion world models mitigate this by generating trajectory segments jointly. However, existing diffusion guides are either policy-only, discarding value information, or reward-based, which becomes myopic when the diffusion horizon is short. We introduce Advantage-Guided Diffusion for MBRL (AGD-MBRL), which steers the reverse diffusion process using the agent's advantage estimates so that sampling concentrates on trajectories expected to yield higher long-term return beyond the generated window. We develop two guides: (i) Sigmoid Advantage Guidance (SAG) and (ii) Exponential Advantage Guidance (EAG). We prove that a diffusion model guided through SAG or EAG allows us to perform reweighted sampling of trajectories with weights increasing in state-action advantage-implying policy improvement under standard assumptions. Additionally, we show that the trajectories generated from AGD-MBRL follow an improved policy (that is, with higher value) compared to an unguided diffusion model. AGD integrates seamlessly with PolyGRAD-style architectures by guiding the state components while leaving action generation policy-conditioned, and requires no change to the diffusion training objective. On MuJoCo control tasks (HalfCheetah, Hopper, Walker2D and Reacher), AGD-MBRL improves sample efficiency and final return over PolyGRAD, an online Diffuser-style reward guide, and model-free baselines (PPO/TRPO), in some cases by a margin of 2x. These results show that advantage-aware guidance is a simple, effective remedy for short-horizon myopia in diffusion-model MBRL.

</details>


### [171] [Mind the Gap Between Spatial Reasoning and Acting! Step-by-Step Evaluation of Agents With Spatial-Gym](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09338)
*Lars Benedikt Kaesberg,Tianyu Yang,Niklas Bauer,Terry Ruas,Jan Philip Wahle,Bela Gipp*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Spatial-Gym 是一个用于评估模型在2D网格路径寻找任务中空间推理能力的Gymnasium环境，采用逐步交互式设置，揭示了现有模型在扩展推理、视觉输入处理和全局规划方面的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的空间推理评测多为单次生成式，无法反映模型在交互环境中逐步推理的能力，且难以诊断具体缺陷；因此需要一个支持序列决策与回溯的标准化测试环境来更准确地衡量模型性能。

Method: 构建名为 Spatial-Gym 的 Gymnasium 环境，设计包含空间约束的2D网格路径寻找任务，评估八种模型在三种模式（单次、逐步、可回溯逐步）下的表现，并与人类、随机及A*算法基线对比。

Result: 最强模型 GPT-OSS 120B 的解决率为16.0%，远低于人类的98.0%；逐步推理对弱模型有帮助（+5.4%），但削弱强模型表现（-5.6%）；回溯提升弱模型完成率，但强模型几乎不使用；视觉输入使解决率下降73%；扩展的思维链推理在逐步模式下仍保持3-5倍优势。

Conclusion: 当前模型在动态调整推理深度、处理视觉空间信息和全局路径规划方面存在显著不足；Spatial-Gym 提供了一个可用于诊断问题并结合强化学习改进空间推理能力的框架。

Abstract: Spatial reasoning is central to navigation and robotics, yet measuring model capabilities on these tasks remains difficult. Existing benchmarks evaluate models in a one-shot setting, requiring full solution generation in a single response, unlike humans, who work in interactive environments step-by-step. We introduce Spatial-Gym, a Gymnasium environment that isolates spatial constraint reasoning by testing pathfinding in 2D-grid puzzles as a sequential decision task with optional backtracking. We evaluate eight models in three settings (one-shot, step-by-step, step-by-step with backtracking) against human, random, and A* baselines on 500 episodes. The best model, GPT-OSS 120B, achieves a solve rate of 16.0%, 82 points below the human baseline (98.0%). Step-by-step format helps weaker models (up to +5.4%) by removing formatting errors, but hurts stronger models (up to 5.6%) by constraining global planning. Backtracking improves episode completion, but increases solve rate only for weaker models; stronger models rarely backtrack and do not benefit from it. Our experiments have three key findings: (1) models fail to scale reasoning effort with difficulty, (2) vision models receiving images of the spatial environment reduce solve rate by 73%, and (3) extended chain-of-thought reasoning retains a 3-5x accuracy advantage over standard inference even in the step-by-step setting. Spatial-Gym enables diagnosis of model limitations and provides a framework for improving spatial reasoning through reinforcement learning.

</details>


### [172] [HiL-Bench (Human-in-Loop Benchmark): Do Agents Know When to Ask for Help?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09408)
*Mohamed Elfeki,Tu Trinh,Kelvin Luu,Guangze Luo,Nathan Hunt,Ernesto Montoya,Nandan Marwaha,Yannis He,Charles Wang,Fernando Crabedo,Alessa Castilo,Bing Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了HiL-Bench，一个用于评估编码智能体在信息不完整或模糊时是否应主动求助的人机协作基准。通过Ask-F1指标衡量智能体的提问质量，揭示了当前前沿模型普遍存在判断力不足的问题，即难以正确判断何时该寻求帮助。实验表明该缺陷是模型级别的共性问题，但可通过基于Ask-F1奖励的强化学习进行训练改善，且能力可跨领域迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准测试仅评估智能体在指令明确情况下的执行能力，忽视其在面对模糊或缺失信息时的判断力（即是否应求助）。这导致模型可能因侥幸猜测成功而被高估，无法反映真实场景中的可靠性。因此需要一种新基准来专门衡量智能体的主动求助能力。

Method: 提出HiL-Bench基准，包含需逐步探索才能发现的人工验证阻碍项（如缺失信息、矛盾要求等），并设计Ask-F1指标（提问精确率与阻碍召回率的调和平均）作为核心评测标准，防止通过滥发问题刷分。在SWE和text-to-SQL两个领域进行评估，并采用基于Ask-F1的强化学习训练模型以提升判断能力。

Result: 评估显示所有前沿模型在不完全信息下性能大幅下降，Ask-F1得分低，暴露出普遍的判断力缺陷。失败模式包括：过度自信且无法察觉知识缺口、虽感知不确定性但仍出错、广泛而不精准地求助且无自我修正。RL训练结果显示32B模型在帮助请求质量和任务通过率上均有提升，且改进效果可跨域迁移，表明模型学会的是识别不可解的不确定性而非特定领域规则。

Conclusion: 智能体在现实场景中缺乏可靠的判断力来决定是否求助，这是当前模型的系统性缺陷。HiL-Bench和Ask-F1为评估和改进这一能力提供了有效框架，证明通过适当训练可以提升模型的自主决策与求助行为，并具备跨领域泛化性。

Abstract: Frontier coding agents solve complex tasks when given complete context but collapse when specifications are incomplete or ambiguous. The bottleneck is not raw capability, but judgment: knowing when to act autonomously and when to ask for help. Current benchmarks are blind to this failure mode. They supply unambiguous detailed instructions and solely reward execution correctness, so an agent that makes a lucky guess for a missing requirement will score identically to one that would have asked to be certain.
  We present HiL-Bench (Human-in-the-Loop Benchmark) to measure this selective escalation skill. Each task contains human-validated blockers (missing information, ambiguous requests, contradictory information) that surface only through progressive exploration, not upfront inspection. Our core metric, Ask-F1, the harmonic mean of question precision and blocker recall, captures the tension between over-asking and silent guessing; its structure architecturally prevents gaming through question spam.
  Evaluation across SWE and text-to-SQL domains reveals a large universal judgment gap: no frontier model recovers more than a fraction of its full-information performance when deciding whether to ask. Failure analysis identifies three key help-seeking patterns: overconfident wrong beliefs with no gap detection; high uncertainty detection yet persistent errors; broad, imprecise escalation without self-correction. These consistent patterns confirm poor help-seeking is a model-level flaw, not task-specific. RL training on shaped Ask-F1 reward shows judgment is trainable: a 32B model improves both help-seeking quality and task pass rate, with gains that transfer across domains. The model does not learn domain-specific heuristics for when to ask; it learns to detect unresolvable uncertainty and act on it.

</details>


### [173] [Do We Really Need to Approach the Entire Pareto Front in Many-Objective Bayesian Optimisation?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09417)
*Chao Jiang,Jingyu Huang,Miqing Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于单点的多目标搜索框架（SPMO），旨在有限评估预算下寻找对决策者最有价值的单一高质量解，而非逼近整个Pareto前沿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多目标贝叶斯优化中，当目标数量较多且评估预算极有限时，传统方法试图逼近整个Pareto前沿变得不现实；而最终决策者仅选用一个解，因此应将重点放在寻找最优单点上。

Method: 提出了SPMO框架及期望单点改进（ESPI）采集函数，适用于无噪声和有噪声场景，并采用样本平均近似（SAA）方法实现基于梯度的有效优化。

Result: 理论证明了ESPI在SAA下的收敛性，实验表明SPMO在多种基准和真实问题上具有计算可行性，并优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: 在极有限的评估预算下，聚焦于寻找高质量单一点比逼近整个Pareto前沿更实用，SPMO为此提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Many-objective optimisation, a subset of multi-objective optimisation, involves optimisation problems with more than three objectives. As the number of objectives increases, the number of solutions needed to adequately represent the entire Pareto front typically grows substantially. This makes it challenging, if not infeasible, to design a search algorithm capable of effectively exploring the entire Pareto front. This difficulty is particularly acute in the Bayesian optimisation paradigm, where sample efficiency is critical and only a limited number of solutions (often a few hundred) are evaluated. Moreover, after the optimisation process, the decision-maker eventually selects just one solution for deployment, regardless of how many high-quality, diverse solutions are available. In light of this, we argue an idea that under a very limited evaluation budget, it may be more useful to focus on finding a single solution of the highest possible quality for the decision-maker, rather than aiming to approximate the entire Pareto front as existing many-/multi-objective Bayesian optimisation methods typically do. Bearing this idea in mind, this paper proposes a \underline{s}ingle \underline{p}oint-based \underline{m}ulti-\underline{o}bjective search framework (SPMO) that aims to improve the quality of solutions along a direction that leads to a good tradeoff between objectives. Within SPMO, we present a simple acquisition function, called expected single-point improvement (ESPI), working under both noiseless and noisy scenarios. We show that ESPI can be optimised effectively with gradient-based methods via the sample average approximation (SAA) approach and theoretically prove its convergence guarantees under the SAA. We also empirically demonstrate that the proposed SPMO is computationally tractable and outperforms state-of-the-arts on a wide range of benchmark and real-world problems.

</details>


### [174] [E3-TIR: Enhanced Experience Exploitation for Tool-Integrated Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09455)
*Weiyang Guo,Zesheng Shi,Liye Zhao,Jiayuan Ma,Zeen Zhu,Junxian He,Min Zhang,Jing Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为E3-TIR的训练范式，旨在提升大语言模型在工具集成推理中的训练效率与性能，通过融合专家前缀、专家引导和自我探索三种经验类型，实现了更高的性能提升和更低的数据成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM在工具集成推理中的训练方法存在探索效率低、模式退化或数据成本高、能力瓶颈等问题，需要一种更高效且低成本的训练范式。

Method: 提出E3-TIR框架，将训练建模为专家前缀、专家引导和自我探索三种经验的动态整合；利用专家路径作为锚点进行分支探索，并采用混合策略优化缓解分布偏移和优化冲突。

Result: 在工具使用任务上比传统方法性能提升6倍，所需合成数据不到10%；在综合考虑性能、数据成本和训练效率的ROI指标上达到基线的1.46倍。

Conclusion: E3-TIR通过有效利用专家经验和自我探索，显著提升了训练效率和模型性能，是一种高性价比的工具集成推理训练新范式。

Abstract: While Large Language Models (LLMs) have demonstrated significant potential in Tool-Integrated Reasoning (TIR), existing training paradigms face significant limitations: Zero-RL suffers from inefficient exploration and mode degradation due to a lack of prior guidance, while SFT-then-RL is limited by high data costs and capability plateaus caused by low-entropy collapse. To address these challenges, we propose E3-TIR (Enhanced Experience Exploitation), a warm-up paradigm for the early stages of agent training. Specifically, we formulate training as the dynamic integration of three experience types: Expert Prefixes, Expert Guided, and Self-Exploration. By executing diverse branching exploration around expert "anchors" and employing a mix policy optimization mechanism, we effectively mitigate distribution shifts and resolve optimization conflicts arising from shared prefixes. Our method dynamically adapts the model's knowledge boundaries, effectively balancing exploration diversity with training efficiency.Experimental results demonstrate that E3-TIR achieves a 6 performance improvement over traditional paradigms on tool-use tasks, while requiring less than 10 of the synthetic data. Furthermore, in terms of ROI, a comprehensive metric integrating performance, data cost, and training efficiency we achieve a 1.46x gain compared to baselines. Code is available at https://github.com/yuki-younai/E3-TIR.

</details>


### [175] [Process Reward Agents for Steering Knowledge-Intensive Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09482)
*Jiwoong Sohn,Tomasz Sternal,Kenneth Styppa,Torsten Hoefler,Michael Moor*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为Process Reward Agents (PRA)的新方法，可在推理过程中为冻结策略模型提供基于领域知识的在线、逐步奖励，从而在医疗等知识密集型领域实现更准确的推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在知识密集型领域中，推理步骤通常无法局部验证，错误容易累积且难以发现。现有的过程奖励模型（PRM）仅在推理完成后进行评分，无法动态干预推理过程。因此需要一种能够在推理过程中实时提供反馈的方法。

Method: 提出Process Reward Agents (PRA)，在测试时利用外部知识源，在每一步生成时对候选推理路径进行打分、排序与剪枝。PRA与检索增强结合，支持搜索式解码，并可与不同规模的冻结策略模型协同工作。

Result: 在多个医学推理基准上实验表明，PRA显著优于现有基线方法，在MedQA数据集上使用Qwen3-4B模型达到80.8%的准确率，创下4B规模下的新纪录；且能泛化到0.5B至8B参数的不同冻结模型，最高提升25.7%的准确率。

Conclusion: PRA实现了推理器与领域奖励模块的解耦，支持无需重新训练即可部署新模型，为复杂领域的推理提供了新范式。

Abstract: Reasoning in knowledge-intensive domains remains challenging as intermediate steps are often not locally verifiable: unlike math or code, evaluating step correctness may require synthesizing clues across large external knowledge sources. As a result, subtle errors can propagate through reasoning traces, potentially never to be detected. Prior work has proposed process reward models (PRMs), including retrieval-augmented variants, but these methods operate post hoc, scoring completed trajectories, which prevents their integration into dynamic inference procedures. Here, we introduce Process Reward Agents (PRA), a test-time method for providing domain-grounded, online, step-wise rewards to a frozen policy. In contrast to prior retrieval-augmented PRMs, PRA enables search-based decoding to rank and prune candidate trajectories at every generation step. Experiments on multiple medical reasoning benchmarks demonstrate that PRA consistently outperforms strong baselines, achieving 80.8% accuracy on MedQA with Qwen3-4B, a new state of the art at the 4B scale. Importantly, PRA generalizes to unseen frozen policy models ranging from 0.5B to 8B parameters, improving their accuracy by up to 25.7% without any policy model updates. More broadly, PRA suggests a paradigm in which frozen reasoners are decoupled from domain-specific reward modules, allowing the deployment of new backbones in complex domains without retraining.

</details>
